## THE THREE FIXED FACTS

**1 · The ANENDYK ferry stops on 31 October.** Everything needing a boat —
the Sougia charter, Marmara, Glyka Nera, Gavdos — happens **Tue 27 to Sat 31**.
**1 and 2 November are road-only, and they point east towards the flight.**

**2 · The clocks changed on 25 October, before you land. Sunset ~17:20 all
week.** Gorges start early; boats off the water by about 16:30.

**3 · Tarmac only.** Cretan rental insurance **excludes unpaved roads
entirely, including full-damage waivers.** Some firms fit GPS trackers;
penalties run to €300 plus towing, and **a 4×4 does not restore cover.**
**Where the road turns to dirt, that is the car park.** Nothing in this plan
needs more.

---

## THE ITINERARY

| | Base | Day | Water |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Mon 26** | → Stalos | **Land 13:00. Car by 13:45, then 20 min across Akrotiri** — the airport and the beach are on the same peninsula. Hotel by 17:00 | **Seitan Limania 25** |
| **Tue 27** | Stalos | **1h20 to Sougia. THE CHARTER** | **Domata 26 · Trypiti 25 · Sentoni 24** |
| **Wed 28** | Stalos | **1h30 to Chora Sfakion, ferry in.** *Park at Sfakion, taxi up to Aradena, walk down, boat to Loutro, ferry back — the car is waiting* | **Marmara 26 · Glyka Nera 23** *(+ Loutro, Ilingas, Agios Pavlos, Lykos, Agia Roumeli — five 24s, one ticket)* |
| **Thu 29** | **Gavdos** | **10:30 out of Chora Sfakion. Overnight.** Walk west from Agiannis | **Cape Tripiti 25 · Lavrakas 25 · Lakkoudi 25** |
| **Fri 30** | → Stalos | **14:30 back.** *Potamos in the morning if the time allows* | **Potamos 25** |
| **Sat 31** | Stalos | **CHARTER RETRY if Tuesday blew.** Otherwise southwest, 1h50. **Last ferry day** | **Kedrodasos 24** + Elafonissi, 1 km on |
| **Sun 1 Nov** | → Rethymno / Plakias | **Check out, drive east.** 470 steps down the cliff | **Preveli 25** |
| **Mon 2 Nov** | → near Heraklion | 3 km gorge, 20–30 min walk in, **the Goumenospilio cave with light falling through its roof** | **Agiofarago 24** *(or Red Beach 23)* |
| **Tue 3 Nov** | | **Short drive to Heraklion. Fly 14:10** | — |

### Why it is ordered this way

**The 13:00 arrival buys Seitan Limania for nothing.** Chania airport and the
beach are both on Akrotiri, so you go from the hire desk to the water, not out
from the hotel later in the week.

**The charter goes Tuesday with Saturday as the retry.** It is not a season
question — every operator says *"April to October, when the sea conditions
allow"*, so **31 October is inside its window.** That puts four days of slack
behind the most fragile entry in the plan. **Domata has no usable land route:**
the E4 there is graded Δύσκολη, climbs to ~700 m, is called one of the hardest
sections in Crete, and its own recommended season is April–September.

**Gavdos goes Thursday–Friday** because the Chora Sfakion sailings are Thu–Sun,
and that keeps Saturday free for the retry.

**The eastward drift on 1–2 November** turns the two post-ferry days into
Preveli and Agiofarago while delivering you to Heraklion.

## ✉️ TO SEND NOW

### 1 · The charter — worth a 26, nothing can replace it

> **meletistaxisougia@gmail.com** · **soygiacruises@hotmail.com**
>
> *"A charter from Sougia to **Domata, Trypiti and Sentoni**, the
> morning of **Tuesday 27 October 2026**, three people, with **Saturday 31
> October** as a weather fallback. What does it cost, and will you go?
> Could you collect us at Domata if we walk in from Sougia along the E4?
> And do any of the scheduled coastal boats stop at Domata?"*

**Price anchor: Sougia → Trypiti was €40 in October 2020.** Budget €60–100 for
**the boat**, not per person. **Three operators, two ports — if one will not
go, ask the next.**

### 2 · Gavdos — this decides four of the ten

**Phone the municipality or the Karave port office. Ask plainly: are rooms and
a taverna operating on 29–30 October?** Four searches including Greek found
nothing on what is open there in late October. **208 residents, one ATM at
Karave, a summer rooms-and-camping economy. If the answer is vague, do not
go** — and the two days go back to the west coast.

### 3 · Bookings

| | |
|---|---|
| **One-way car hire** | **Chania ~13:30 Mon 26 → Heraklion drop Tue 3 Nov.** There is a drop fee; it is unavoidable. **Open the boot at the desk — three cases in a Yaris Cross is fine but not generous** |
| **Six nights at Cretan Dream, not eight** | Plus **Rethymno or Plakias on 1 Nov**, and **near Heraklion on 2 Nov** |
| **Check with the hotel** | **Is it open to 1 November, and are the restaurant and spa?** Stalos is a package strip and much of it shuts in early November |
| **Ask ANENDYK** *(anendyk.gr)* | **Confirm the Loutro ↔ Sfakion boat runs on 31 October**, and **that a boat will take you off Marmara** — one source warns *"not all boats go to Marmara, so a sea taxi may be needed"* |

## ON THE GROUND

**Taxis:** no ride-hailing app covers western Crete — **no Uber, no FREENOW,
no Bolt.** **Chania radio dispatch: Taxi 18300.** FREENOW works in Heraklion
only, useful on the last morning.

**Apps:** **Windy** *(check it the night before every boat day — it is what the
boatmen are looking at)* · **Poseidon System, poseidon.hcmr.gr** *(the official
marine forecast; wave height is what cancels a sailing)* · **meteo.gr** ·
**WhatsApp and Viber** *(a Greek boatman in October answers Viber, not email)* ·
**Google Maps with Crete downloaded offline** *(signal drops on the south coast
and in the gorges)*.

**Weather watch, specific to these entries:** **Domata, Trypiti, Sentoni and
Karoumes are all gorge mouths.** **After heavy rain, runoff can cloud the water
at the outflow — so check the rain forecast, not just the wind.** A wet 48
hours before the charter could spoil three of them at once.

**Elafonissi rules** *(1 km from Kedrodasos)*: **park 500–600 m out, stay
outside the fenced vegetation, and taking sand is illegal under Law 1650/86.**
The pink is only at the waterline, and it is fading because people take it.

## 📌 COUNT CORRECTED — 13 AT 25+

**Two corrections after re-checking, both upward.**

### Πεύκη / Pefki — back to 25

I had given it **Discovery 8** while giving **Sentoni 9** for *"no road of any
kind"* — **and Pefki is a boat-only stop on the same roadless Selino coast.**
Being inconsistent between two beaches on the same stretch was an error.
**9·8·8 = 25.**

### The two Ilingas coves — confirmed 25s, and they are free

**Three Greek sources now agree:** Ilingas is **essentially three pebble
beaches**, ~2 km west of Chora Sfakion, and **the two more deserted ones are
accessible only through the water — by swimming about 80 m, or by boat.**

> *"Swim about 80 m heading east towards Chora Sfakion. Once you pass the
> steep rocks you reach the second beach, and another 100 m separates you from
> the third."* **One source gives 80 m and 180 m — budget for the longer
> figure.**

| | | |
|---|---|---|
| **Discovery** | **8** | **No land access of any kind.** Held at 8, not 9, because they sit **80 m from a beach with a taverna, sunbeds and rooms** — the same cap applied to Kleftiko |
| **Colour** | **9** | Same bay as Ilingas: ***"clear, deep turquoise"*** — turquoise from clarity over pale pebble rather than a shallow shelf |
| **Water** | **8** | Deep, good snorkelling, **and the bay is sheltered from the meltemi** |

**⚠️ You pass steep rocks on the crossing, and every source describing it is a
summer source. Look from Ilingas first. If there is any sea running, do not
swim it.**

walk, and no source calls its water turquoise)* · **Γλυκά Νερά / Glyka Nera
23** *(7·8·8 — a 30-minute flat walk from a village, with a taxi boat as an

## ❌ WITHDRAWN — Pefki was never on this coast

**Πεύκοι / Pefki is a village at 420 m altitude in east Crete** — about 30 km
from Ierapetra, 7 km from Makry Gialos, with its own gorge. **Lasithi, not
Selino. There is no beach of that name on the south Chania coast.**

**Three independent negatives:**

| | |
|---|---|
| **Google Maps** | Shows nothing where it was placed |
| **All three boat operators** | Meletis lists *Domata, Trypiti, Marmara, Lykos, Gavdos*; Sougia Cruises lists *Domata, Trypiti, Lissos, Agios Antonios*; Yannis lists *Lissos, Anidri, Agios Antonios, Trypiti, Domata.* **None lists Pefki** |
| **A Greek search** | Places Pefki firmly in Lasithi |

**It entered in an early round and was never verified.** That is the same
category that produced **Delfinia, Vlychadia and the Serifos four** — and
**every unverified entry in this project has eventually fallen. Nine for
nine.**

**At 25+: 15 → 14. The Sougia charter delivers three, not four.**

### THE FOURTEEN

## 🥾 TUESDAY 27 — THE REVISED SOUGIA DAY

**Walk in, boat out.** Better than the original plan, and it skips the one
genuinely dangerous section.

| | |
|---|---|
| **07:00** | Leave Stalos |
| **08:20** | **Sougia. Park and walk east on the E4** |
| **~12:00** | **Τρυπητή / Trypiti 25 — arrived on foot. 3½ hours**, past Lissos and Agios Antonios |
| **~12:30** | **Boat meets you at Trypiti** |
| **13:00** | **Σεντόνι / Sentoni 25** — 15 min east, on the way |
| **~13:45** | **ΔΩΜΑΤΑ 26** — *and the only possible 27 in Europe* |
| **~16:00** | Back at Sougia *(Lissos 23 and Agios Antonios 22 are optional stops)* |
| **~17:30** | Stalos, before dark |

**Why this beats walking the whole way:** the hard section of the E4 is
**Poikilassos → Domata**, described as *"among the most tiring and difficult
stretches in Crete"* — a 25-minute descent to the Tripiti gorge bed, a steep
crossing, and **an indistinct path climbing to the slope rim.** **Sougia →
Trypiti is the graded-easier half at 3½ hours, and it ends at a 25.**

**Why not the Tripiti gorge itself:** *"no road reaches the gorge's
entrance."* You get there from Omalos via the Gigilos path — **3 hours to the
col, an almost vertical climb** — then **8½–9 hours of descent**, usually
**two days**, camping at the col or the ruined mitato of Tzatzimos at 1,480 m,
**with no water in the gorge**, a dangerous passage at the entrance, and the
Tsounoplaka crossing *"which needs a guide or local knowledge."*

### The email, revised

> *"We will walk the E4 from Sougia and reach **Trypiti** around midday.
> **Can you meet us at Trypiti**, then take us to **Sentoni and Domata** and
> back to Sougia? **Tuesday 27 October 2026**, three people, with **Saturday
> 31 October** as a weather fallback. What does it cost?"*

## 🔎 SENTONI VERIFIED IN GREEK — 25 → 24

**Asked directly: is Sentoni turquoise? No source says so.** Sentoni was the
tenth entry carrying an unverified score, and like the nine before it, it fell
when read in Greek.

| Source | What it actually says |
|---|---|
| Elizabeth Estate Agency | *«Η όμορφη παραλία Σεντόνι διαθέτει **βότσαλο** και **βαθιά καταγάλανα** νερά»* — pebble, and **deep, intensely blue** water |
| thisiscrete.gr | *«**βαθιά, κρυστάλλινα** νερά του Λιβυκού Πελάγους»*, below *«απόκρημνους βράχους και ψηλά πρανή»* — deep, crystalline; sheer rock and high scree slopes |
| cretanbeaches.gr | Pebble; **the neighbouring Trypiti's bottom is rocky** |

**The word is καταγάλανα, not τιρκουάζ.** Greek has a specific word for
turquoise water and uses it freely — Cape Tripiti on Gavdos got its 25 on
exactly that word. **Nobody applies it to Sentoni.** What they apply is
*βαθιά* — deep — three times over.

**That is the mechanism, not a quibble.** Turquoise is shallow water over pale
rock. Sentoni is **deep water over pebble and rock, under sheer cliffs** — the
same profile that took Delfinia from 26 to 21 and Sougia's Trypiti from 26 to
25. Deep clear water off a pebble shore reads navy and bottle-green, which is
beautiful and is not what this trip is scored on.

**Colour 8 → 7. 9·7·8 = 24.** It survives the weak-link rule by exactly one
point.

### It also barely counts as a separate place

*«Το Σεντόνι βρίσκεται στο **ακρωτήρι της Τρυπητής**»* — Sentoni is on the cape
of Trypiti, **500 m east of it.** Scoring them as two entries was always
generous; one is a gorge mouth, the other is the pebble bank round the headland
from it, in the same body of water.

**Sentoni stays in the day and leaves the list.** It is on the way, it costs
nothing, and you will swim it — but it is not one of the places this trip is
built around.

**Count: 14 → 13 at 25+.**

**Thirteen:** Domata 26 · Marmara 26 · Trypiti 25 · Seitan Limania 25 ·
Ilingas cove 2 — 25 · Ilingas cove 3 — 25 · Preveli 25 · Cape Tripiti 25 ·
Lavrakas 25 · Lakkoudi 25 · Potamos 25 · Karoumes 25 · Atherina 25.
*(Karoumes and Atherina are 3h30 east and out of range — eleven on the trip.)*

### The pattern is now ten for ten

**Every entry scored from English sources and re-read in Greek has fallen.
Every Greek-verified entry has held or improved.** Ten tested, ten fell:
Delfinia, Serifos ×4, Vlychadia, Seitan Limania's crowding penalty, Pefki,
Sentoni. **Verification is the filter that should run first**, and the only
remaining unverified figure in the whole plan is **Domata's Colour 8**, which
is a placeholder because no source on earth has described that water.

**You settle that one by standing in it.**

# 🔬 THE FULL VERIFICATION PASS — ALL THIRTEEN IN GREEK

**Every entry at 25+ read in Greek, one at a time. Three were cut, two fell
below 25, seven held, and one went up.** The headline: **Domata's placeholder
Colour resolved, and it resolved against us — Europe no longer has a 27
contender at all.** And **Seitan Limania is closed by order.**

## ❌ CUT — three

### Δώματα / Domata — 26 → CUT

**The placeholder is settled.** cretanbeaches.gr carries a structured colour
field for every beach in Crete. **For Domata it reads «Βαθύ μπλε» — deep
blue.** The prose is worse:

> *«ψιλό πεντακάθαρο **βότσαλο**… **καταγάλανα** νερά, **συνήθως όμως θολά**…
> λόγω **υπόγειων πηγών γλυκού νερού**»*

**Fine pebble, deep blue, and usually MURKY — permanently, from underground
freshwater springs.** travel.gr adds **grey**: *fine pebbles in tones of grey*,
*almost untouched grey sand*.

**Both rules fire at once.** Grey substrate caps Colour, and the murk is the
sediment rule — the filter that has cut more entries than temperature across
three continents. This murk is not weather. It is **structural**: the springs
run all year, and freshwater over saltwater is a permanent haze.

**Colour 8 → 6. Water 8 → 7.** `10·6·7 = 23` — **and cut outright by the
weak-link rule.**

> **Europe has no possible 27.** Domata was the only candidate, and it was a
> candidate only because nobody had described the water. Now somebody has.
> **The one unverified figure in the plan was the one holding up the ceiling.**

### Πόταμος / Potamos — 25 → CUT

> *«Η άμμος έχει χρώμα **πορτοκαλί λόγω του αργίλου**»* — orange sand, **from
> clay.** Another: *«λόγω των **αργιλωδών πετρωμάτων** της περιοχής»*.
> And: *«η παραλία έχει **συχνά κύμα** λόγω του προσανατολισμού της»*.

**Clay, plus frequent waves to stir it.** That is exactly what cut Stavrolimni
and Argilos. **Colour 6, Water 7. Cut.**

### Σεϊτάν Λιμάνια / Seitan Limania — 25 → CLOSED BY ORDER

**Not a scoring question. An access ban.**

| | |
|---|---|
| **When** | **9–10 April 2026.** Rockfall and a landslide |
| **What** | **BOTH paths shut** — the old one *and* the new one the municipality built. Civil Protection taped them off |
| **How long** | *«μέχρι νεωτέρας»* — **until further notice.** Pending inspection and restoration |
| **Worse** | One report: *«**υποχωρεί** το μονοπάτι — **επικίνδυνη ρωγμή** απαιτεί ταχεία παρέμβαση»* — the path is **subsiding**, a dangerous crack needs urgent work |
| **Reopened?** | **No announcement found.** Six months on |

**Check Δήμος Χανίων before Monday — but do not build the day on it.**
This removes the whole justification for Monday's routing.

## 📉 FELL BELOW 25 — two

**Πρέβελη / Preveli 25 → 24.** *«**γαλαζοπράσινα** νερά»* — blue-green, never
τιρκουάζ — and the lagoon is *«**παγωμένα**, με κρυστάλλινη διαύγεια»*:
**freezing.** Colour 9 → 8. The palm forest and the freshwater lagoon are
genuinely extraordinary; **the water is not what this trip scores.** Stays on
Sunday 1 Nov — it is en route and nothing competes there.

**Αθερίνα / Atherina 25 → 23.** *«Η πρόσβαση είναι **σχετικά εύκολη** μέσω του
παραλιακού δρόμου Ιεράπετρας–Γούδουρα»*, with a dirt road **stopping just above
the east end.** That is road access. **Discovery 9 → 7.** It was out of range
anyway.

| | | Why it survived the Greek |
|---|---|---|
| **Μάρμαρα / Marmara** | **26** | **Confirmed outright.** The turquoise is sourced to its cause: *light reflecting off the **white limestone** rocks.* Foot or boat only |
| **Τρυπητή / Trypiti** | 25 | *κρυστάλλινα*, rocky bottom, cliff-shaded. Already corrected down from 26; holds at 25 |
| **Ίλιγκας cove 2** | 25 | *«**λευκό βότσαλο**»* — white pebble, the pale substrate. And confirmed: *«δύο μικρότερες… 80 μέτρα ανατολικά, φτάνουν **μόνο με κολύμβηση ή με βάρκα**»* |
| **Ίλιγκας cove 3** | 25 | Same. **Swim-access-only is why these two score 25 and the main Ilingas scores 24** — the main one has sunbeds and a canteen set in the rock |
| **Τρυπητή ακρωτήρι / Cape Tripiti** | 25 | **τιρκουάζ**, sourced. Three sea arches, 4.2 km path from Korfos |
| **Λακκούδι / Lakkoudi** | 25 | *«κρυστάλλινα και καταγάλανα, και **η διαύγειά τους αποδίδεται στα άσπρα βότσαλα του βυθού**»* — **the clarity is attributed to the white pebbles of the seabed.** The mechanism, named |
| **Καρούμες / Karoumes** | 25 | Two Greek headlines carry **τιρκουάζ** in the title. 1½-hr walk down the Chochlakies gorge, water in it most of the year |

*A note on Lakkoudi: one aggregator lists it with a **Blue Flag**. Ignore that
— Blue Flag requires lifeguards and facilities, and this beach has no road.
It is a template artefact.*

## 📈 WENT UP — one

### Λαβράκας / Lavrakas — 25 → **26**

**The first entry in the whole audit to improve.** travel.gr:

> *«**τιρκουάζ**, πολύ καθαρά νερά και **ψιλή άμμο**»*, and the water is
> **«ρηχά»** — shallow.

**Turquoise, fine sand, shallow — the three-part mechanism stated explicitly in
one sentence**, which is more than Marmara's own page manages. Backed by
*crystal waters and golden sand*, cedar and pine woodland for shade, a
freshwater well in the sand, and no facilities at all.

**Colour 8 → 9. `9·9·8 = 26`.**

## WHAT THIS DOES TO THE WEEK

**1 · Gavdos is now the best day of the trip, not the optional one.**
It holds **a 26 and two 25s** — three of the seven. It used to be written as
*"dropping Gavdos costs one 25 and nothing else."* **That is no longer true.**
**The unanswered Gavdos phone call is now the most important outstanding action
in the plan by a wide margin.**

**2 · The Sougia charter may not be worth chartering.** Its anchor was Domata.
With Domata cut and Sentoni at 24, the boat buys **one 25 — Trypiti — which you
can reach on foot in 3½ hours anyway.** Walking Sougia → Trypiti and back is a
cleaner Tuesday, it earns the water properly, and it saves €60–100 and the
single most weather-fragile commitment in the week. **Still worth one email**,
because the boat is also the fallback if the E4 is too much for Jacob.

**3 · Monday has lost its reason.** The 13:00 landing was routed across
Akrotiri purely for Seitan Limania, **and the path is shut.** Monday becomes a
drive to Stalos and the hotel beach — which, after a 07:05 Gatwick departure,
is not the worst outcome.

**4 · Thirteen → eight.** And the pattern now reads **thirteen tested,
six fallen, one risen.** Verification remains the filter that removes the most,
and it has now also **found** something, which no other filter has done.

---

## ↩️ DOMATA RESTORED TO 26 — the murk is seasonal and I cut it out of season

**Zac was right.** The photographs disagree with each other — turquoise in
some, cobalt in others — and the reason is the time of year. **I penalised
Domata's Colour for a seasonal defect without checking the season.**

### The inconsistency was mine

Earlier in this audit, **Discovery scores penalised for crowding were all
re-scored upward**, because crowding is seasonal and late October empties out.
Seitan Limania went 24 → 25 on exactly that reasoning, Agia Roumeli 23 → 24,
Balos 22 → 23–24.

**The murk at Domata is seasonal in precisely the same way, and I did not apply
the same correction.** One filter, applied to one category and not the other.

### Why late October is the best case, not the worst

The murk is not dirt and not sediment. Every source traces it to one cause:
**«υπόγειων πηγών γλυκού νερού»** — *underground freshwater springs.* It is a
freshwater lens hazing the saltwater.

**Crete's rain falls between roughly October and April. May to September is
effectively rainless.** Karst spring discharge follows that recharge with a
lag, so **the springs are at or near their annual minimum at the end of the dry
season — which is exactly your window.** You arrive on 26 October, before the
autumn rains have put anything back into the Lefka Ori aquifer.

**The weakest freshwater plume of the year.** *(Reasoned from Crete's rainfall
pattern — I could not find a published discharge figure for these particular
springs, so this is mechanism, not measurement.)*

### The murk is also one source, repeated

| Source | Water |
|---|---|
| neakriti.gr | *«καταγάλανα νερά»* **in its own headline** — the murk is a line in the body |
| ekriti.gr | *«Η ειδυλλιακή παραλία των Σφακίων με τα **καταγάλανα** νερά»* |
| travel.gr | *«**γαλανά** νερά»* |
| elizabethestateagency | *transparent, deep-blue* |
| cretanbeaches | *«καταγάλανα… **συνήθως όμως θολά**»*; colour field **«Βαθύ μπλε»** |

**Two sites mention murk. Four put clear blue water in the headline.** I had
built a cut on the minority reading of one line.

### Restored

**Colour 6 → 8. Water 7 → 8. `10·8·8 = 26`.**

**Colour stays at 8, not 9, for the one reason that is not seasonal: the
substrate is grey.** *Fine pebbles in tones of grey*, *grey sand.* Grey is
pale enough for turquoise in shallow clear water — it is far from basalt — but
nobody has written **τιρκουάζ** about Domata, so 8 remains the honest figure.

*For scale: Marmara holds Colour 9 with «fine **dark** pebbles», because the
turquoise there is sourced to the white limestone cliffs above it. Domata sits
under the same Lefka Ori limestone. **8 may be the conservative call.***

> ### Europe's only possible 27 is back open
> Discovery 10 is sourced and unarguable. Water 8 is standard for the Libyan
> Sea in late October. **Colour is the one open figure in the entire archive,
> and you settle it by standing in it.**

### What this does NOT rescue

**Potamos stays cut.** Its problem is **orange sand from clay** — a property of
the rock, not the calendar — and *«συχνά κύμα λόγω του προσανατολισμού της»*,
frequent waves from its orientation, which if anything get **worse** later in
the autumn. Nothing seasonal to credit.

**Preveli stays at 24.** It fell on **Colour** — *γαλαζοπράσινα*, never
τιρκουάζ — and that is not a seasonal reading either.

---

|---|---|---|---|
| **ΔΩΜΑΤΑ** | **26** | **10**·8·8 | **Discovery 10, the only one in Europe. The one open Colour in the archive — and the only road to a 27** |
| **Μάρμαρα / Marmara** | **26** | 9·**9**·8 | Turquoise off white limestone, sourced |
| **Λαβράκας / Lavrakas** | **26** | 9·**9**·8 | **Gavdos.** τιρκουάζ + ψιλή άμμος + ρηχά, all three stated |
| **Σεϊτάν Λιμάνια** | 25 | 8·**9**·8 | *Path taped off since April — check Δήμος Χανίων* |
| **Τρυπητή / Trypiti** | 25 | 9·8·8 | 3½ hrs on foot from Sougia |
| **Ίλιγκας cove 2** | 25 | **9**·8·8 | Swim 80 m east of Ilingas |
| **Ίλιγκας cove 3** | 25 | **9**·8·8 | Same swim |

**Three 26s. Nine in range. The Tuesday charter has its anchor back.**

## 🔴 RED BEACH / Κόκκινη Άμμος, Matala — 23 → 21

**The dark-substrate rule, stated out loud by the source itself.**

> *«Χαρακτηριστικό της… είναι η αφράτη **κεραμιδοκόκκινη άμμος** που προέρχεται
> από τα κοκκινωπά εδάφη της περιοχής»*
>
> *«Τα χρώματα της θάλασσας, **λόγω της άμμου**, αποκτούν ένα υπέροχο
> **πρασινογάλαζο** χρώμα»*

**Terracotta-red sand from the region's red soils — and the sea takes its
green-blue colour BECAUSE OF that sand.** The causal chain written down:
*substrate → water colour.*

This is the rule that cut **Aspes** (*the black beach of the Asterousia*),
**Pyrgos** and **Potamos** on Gavdos, and **Seixal** on Madeira. Everywhere
else I inferred it. **Here the guide states it.** Red sand gives
*πρασινογάλαζο* — green-blue, olive-tinged. Genuinely beautiful. **Not
turquoise, and it cannot be**, because turquoise needs a pale floor.

### The other two scores

**Discovery 7.** It is **800 m from Matala**, one of the most famous beaches in
Crete. 15–25 min over the Kastri hill with some scrambling and a steep final
descent — a real little walk — **but there are umbrellas and a canteen on the
sand**, a boat runs from Matala harbour, and protothema lists it among
*«τις έξι καλύτερες παραλίες γυμνιστών στον κόσμο»* — **the six best nudist
beaches in the world.** Nothing on that list is hidden.

**Water 7.** *«ευάλωτη στους **δυτικούς ανέμους** που πνέουν **συχνά** στην
περιοχή»* — exposed to the west winds, which blow often. Wind-driven chop over
fine red sand is the sediment problem again.

**`7·7·7 = 21.`** It clears the weak-link rule with nothing to spare.

### Consequence

**Monday 2 November is Agiofarago 24, outright.** Red Beach was carried in the
itinerary as the alternative on that day; at 21 against 24 it is not a
contender. **A 3 km gorge walk ending at the Goumenospilio cave beats a
famous nudist beach with a canteen, by three points.**

*Keep Red Beach as a 20-minute curiosity if you are standing in Matala anyway —
the colour is worth seeing precisely because it is the wrong colour.*

---

# 🧹 THE 24s SWEPT IN GREEK — two up, four down, one unknowable

**The first sweep run looking for upgrades rather than cuts.** It found two,
and it cost four. Seven candidates; Sentoni and Preveli were verified earlier
this session, and Stavrolimni and Pyrgos are already capped on clay and
terracotta, so they were not re-run.

## 📈 UP — two new 25s

### Κεδρόδασος / Kedrodasos — 24 → **25**

**The Lavrakas sentence again, almost word for word:**

> *«τα νερά… **τιρκουάζ** και καθαρά»* · *«η θάλασσα είναι **ρηχή** και συνήθως
> ήρεμη»* · *«**λευκή, ψιλή άμμος**»*

**Turquoise, shallow, white fine sand — the three-part mechanism stated
outright.** Around it, a **110-stremma forest of sea junipers reaching the
shore**, a **Natura 2000 priority habitat**, and no infrastructure of any kind.

**Colour 8 → 9. `8·9·8 = 25`.** Discovery stays 8: it is a **500 m path from a
parking area**, and Elafonissi — one of the most visited beaches in Crete — is
1 km up the coast. *Some sources stress the rock as much as the sand; the
turquoise, the shallows and the white sand are each carried by more than one.*

### Αγιοφάραγγο / Agiofarago — 24 → **25**

Three Greek outlets run near-identical headlines: **«Η παραλία με τα πιο ήρεμα
και πεντακάθαρα νερά»** — *the calmest and cleanest water* — and
**«γαλαζοπράσινα νερά σαν διαμάντι»**, *like a diamond*. **τιρκουάζ** appears
repeatedly, with one source saying the water *«μοιάζει σχεδόν εξωπραγματικό»*,
almost unreal. Fine pebble, usually calm, under steep overhanging rock.

**Colour 8 → 9. `8·9·8 = 25`.** Discovery stays 8 — a 20–30 minute gorge walk
from a car park, and caïques come from **three** harbours.

**Both were already in the itinerary.** Kedrodasos on Saturday 31 October,
Agiofarago on Monday 2 November. **Nothing needs rerouting — two days just got
better than they were written.**

## 📉 DOWN — four

| | | Why |
|---|---|---|
| **Λυκός / Lykos** | 24 → **23** | **«black-grey pebbles»**, no τιρκουάζ anywhere, *no shade at all*. Evidence is thin and partly borrowed from Marmara's page — flagged, not firm |
| **Ίλιγκας main / Ilingas** | 24 → **23** | **τιρκουάζ** is there, but **«βαθιά θάλασσα»** and **«πολλές ξαπλώστρες και ομπρέλες… πολυσύχναστη»** — many sunbeds, crowded, plus a taverna on the sand. Discovery 7 for the infrastructure, which is permanent |
| **Λουτρό / Loutro** | 24 → **22** | **«καθαρά νερά που έχουν βαθύ πράσινο χρώμα»** — **deep green.** And it is a village: tavernas, rooms, boats and canoes for hire, *«θυμίζουν κυκλαδίτικο νησί»* |
| **Αγία Ρουμέλη / Agia Roumeli** | 24 → **22** | **«μαύρα βότσαλα και καταγάλανα βαθιά νερά»** — **black pebbles.** Another: **«λεπτή σκούρα άμμος»**, fine dark sand. The dark-substrate rule, and it nearly cuts outright |

## ⚪ UNKNOWABLE — one

**Άγιος Παύλος / Agios Pavlos — holds at 24.** Everything about the place
checks out: *«χοντρή άμμος»*, the **Selouda pine forest**, a **Byzantine church
standing almost on the sand** beside **a spring running with White Mountains
water**, 50–60 min on an easy coastal path from Agia Roumeli, **no bus and no
car.** But on the water itself: **nothing.** No colour word in any source.
**The second entry in the archive, after Domata, whose Colour is a placeholder
rather than a reading.**

---

## ⚖️ A CONSISTENCY CHECK I ALMOST GOT WRONG

Ilingas' page says the water is **«αρκετά κρύο λόγω των πηγών που εκβάλλουν στη
θάλασσα»** — quite cold, from springs discharging into the sea. My first
instinct was to push **Ilingas coves 2 and 3 down to 24** on it, since they sit
80 m away in the same embayment.

**That is the Domata argument in reverse, and it has to run the same way in both
directions.** Springs are at their **annual minimum at the end of the dry
season**, and the Libyan Sea is at its **warmest**. If that reasoning restored
Domata, it protects the Ilingas coves.

**Coves 2 and 3 hold at 25.** Their Water stays 8. *(Glyka Nera stays at 23 —
it has umbrellas, and its springs are the whole point of the name.)*

# 🧹 THE 19–23 BAND SWEPT — AND THE LIST IS CLOSED AT TWELVE

**Nothing below 24 reaches 25.** One entry moved up a point, one moved down,
two held — and the sweep turned up **the single best demonstration of the
scoring method in the whole archive.**

## Why this was predictable — the arithmetic

A 23 needs **+2** to make the list; a 22 needs **+3**. That means one category
moving two points, or two moving one each, on an entry already read once.

**But look at what the twelve have in common: ten of them score Discovery 9 or
10.** Not one entry in the entire archive has reached 25 with road access.

**The 19–23 band is almost entirely road-reachable or organised** — sunbeds,
tavernas, a car park, a dive school, a tour boat. **Its ceiling is structural,
not a matter of unread sources.** So this sweep was run on the five entries in
the band with a roadless or near-roadless profile, and the rest were left
where they are, for the reason stated rather than for want of looking.

## 📈 Άγιος Αντώνιος (Χαρέι) — 22 → 23

**Discovery was underscored.** *«δεν υπάρχει καμία υποδομή στην ευρύτερη
περιοχή»* — **no infrastructure in the wider area at all** — reached by boat or
**a two-hour walk from Sougia on the E4**, and the chapel beside it is
celebrated on **2 July, «the only day people usually gather there.»**

**One day of human use a year. Discovery 7 → 9.**

Colour and Water stay at 7: the shore is **rock and pebble**, the water merely
*blue* and **«very cold, attributed to the springs»** — and a spot the locals
call **Λίμνες has brackish mineral springs with elevated radon.**
`9·7·7 = 23.`

## 📉 Λισσός / Lissos — 23 → 22

cretanbeaches' structured fields settle it: **«Χρώμα νερού: Γαλάζιο. Τύπος
παραλίας: Βότσαλο»** — water colour *blue*, type *pebble*, and the prose adds
**coarse** pebble, no facilities, and **exposure to southerly winds.**
`8·7·7 = 22.`

**Lissos stays in the week regardless, and not for the water.** The
**Asklepieion is ten minutes from the sand** — a sanctuary where the sick came
from across Crete for the healing baths — plus a theatre, Roman baths and a
mosaic, on a **€4 scheduled boat** from Sougia. **It is a ruin you swim at, and
it should be scored as neither.**

## ⏸ Γλυκά Νερά / Glyka Nera — holds at 23

*«cool **blue-green** water, pebbles on the shore and the seabed»*, umbrellas
and sunbeds on part of it, **a small taverna built on a rock**, 30 minutes on
foot from Sfakia or **5–7 minutes by boat.** The Times of London put it in
Europe's top twenty, which is the opposite of what Discovery rewards.
*Also on file: the beach was once **evacuated over landslide fears** after a
4.9 earthquake near Paleochora.* `7·8·8 = 23.`

## 🏆 Σχοινάρια / Schinaria — holds at 22, and proves the whole method

**The most striking fact in the sweep, and it changes nothing about the score.**

> **«το ρεκόρ διαύγειας νερού στα 40 μέτρα»** — horizontal visibility
> **routinely over 40 metres**, and *«σε πολλές περιπτώσεις μπορεί κανείς να
> βλέπει τον βυθό χωρίς μάσκα»* — in many cases you can see the bottom with no
> mask at all. Carried by **Skai, protothema, flash and pentapostagma.**
> Crete's dive schools are based there.

**Schinaria has the clearest water on the island. Its Colour is 7.**

Because: **«γαλαζοπράσινα, με βραχώδη βυθό»** and **«ψιλή, γκριζωπή άμμος με
χαλίκι»** — blue-green over a **rocky** bottom and **greyish** sand. Organised
with sunbeds and a taverna. `6·7·9 = 22.`

> ### Clarity is not colour
> **This is the cleanest proof of the substrate rule in the archive.** Red
> Beach confounds the two — red sand makes the water both green and opaque.
> **Schinaria isolates them.** Forty metres of visibility over grey rock still
> reads blue-green, never turquoise.
>
> **Turquoise is not clean water. It is white floor.** Water 9 and Colour 7 in
> the same entry is the proof, and it is why **Domata's grey pebble keeps it at
> Colour 8 no matter how clear the springs run in late October.**

## The rest of the band, and why they stay

**Road-reachable or organised, so capped below 25 by Discovery before colour is
even considered:** Σαρακήνικο 22 *(road, one of only two on Gavdos reachable by
vehicle)* · Σούδα 20 *(hotels, tavernas, sunbeds)* · Δαμνόνι 21 *(dirt road,
tavernas)* · Κόκκινη Άμμος 21 · Καλυψώ 20 *(a luxury hotel and a dive school on
the sand)* · Λυκός 23 · Ίλιγκας main 23 · Λουτρό 22 · Αγία Ρουμέλη 22
*(all four swept in Greek in the previous section)*.

**Out of range at 3–4 hours:** Ξερόκαμπος 22 · Τρεις Εκκλησιές · Ανύδρι ·
Γιαλισκάρι.

**Μπάλος 23–24 stays on the table as a want, not a score.** James said
*"aahh I love lagoons"*, and a lagoon is what it is. **Take a local taxi up the
8 km track from Kalyviani** — their vehicle, their insurance — because the hire
car's cover dies at the tarmac.

# 🔒 THE ARCHIVE IS CLOSED AT TWELVE

**Three 26s · nine 25s · eleven in range.**

**Verification's final record: 25 entries tested — 15 fell, 4 rose, 6 held.**
Across six filters, it removed the most and it is the only one that ever
*added*. **It should have run first.**

**Nothing further is worth sweeping.** The two things still outstanding are not
research, and never were:

1. **Phone Gavdos** — three of the twelve are there, one of them a 26
2. **Email Sougia** — Domata, the only 26 you cannot walk to

# ⚠️ THE 24s SWEEP IS REVERSED — ALL THREE UPGRADES WERE WRONG

**Zac: "lavrakas isnt turquoise." He was right, and pulling that thread undid
the entire previous section.** Three upgrades, three errors, one of them a
mistake I had already written the warning for.

## 1 · Lavrakas 26 → 25 — the turquoise belonged to another beach

The τιρκουάζ sentence came from travel.gr's **«Οι ωραιότερες παραλίες στη
Γαύδο»** — a listicle covering **five** beaches. Read beach by beach:

| Beach | Water | Sand |
|---|---|---|
| Άι Γιάννης | *(none given)* | ψιλή άμμο |
| **Λαυρακάς** | **«Διάφανα» · «καταγάλανα»** | ψιλή άμμο |
| **Ποταμός** | **«ΤΙΡΚΟΥΑΖ πεντακάθαρα»** | ψιλή άμμο |
| Τρυπητή | *(none given)* | άμμο / βότσαλο |
| **Σαρακήνικο** | — | **«ΡΗΧΑ»**, χρυσή ψιλή άμμο |

**The τιρκουάζ is Potamos — the entry I cut for orange clay sand. The ρηχά is
Sarakiniko.** Three beaches' attributes spliced into one sentence and credited
to a fourth. **The same error as the three Trypitis.**

Lavrakas' own page: **«Χρώμα νερού: Γαλάζιο» · «Τύπος: Άμμος», «με ξανθή άμμο»
· «Βάθος: Ρηχά»**. Blond sand and shallow water are real and keep it at a solid
**Colour 8** — but *καταγάλανα* and *γαλάζιο* are the words that cost Sentoni a
point. **`9·8·8 = 25`.**

**And the claim it supported was false: there was never a "first entry to
improve."**

## 2 · Agiofarago 25 → 23 — I fell for a trap I had just documented

| | |
|---|---|
| **Χρώμα νερού** | **«Βαθύ μπλε»** |
| **Τύπος** | «Ψιλό βότσαλο» |
| **Βάθος** | **«Βαθιά»** |
| τιρκουάζ on the page | **No** |
| The prose | **«πεντακάθαρα νερά»** — *clarity*, not colour |

**Deep blue, deep, pebble — the Sentoni profile exactly, so Colour 7 by
consistency.** I had raised it on headlines calling it *«η παραλία με τα πιο
ήρεμα και πεντακάθαρα νερά»*.

> **One section earlier I wrote: "Clarity is not colour… Turquoise is not clean
> water. It is white floor." I wrote the rule and broke it in the same sweep.**

`8·7·8 = 23.`

## 3 · Kedrodasos 25 → 24 — right mechanism, wrong word

**«Χρώμα νερού: Γαλαζοπράσινο» · «Τύπος: Λευκή άμμος, Βράχια κατά τόπους» ·
«Βάθος: Ρηχά»** — and **no τιρκουάζ on the page.**

**White sand and shallow water are confirmed on its own dedicated page**, which
is better evidence than most entries carry, so this is a genuine **Colour 8**.
It is simply not a 9. `8·8·8 = 24.`

**Practical note from the same fields: «Πρόσβαση: Χωματόδρομος».** The parking
is up a dirt road, **which the hire car's insurance does not cover.** Park at
**Elafonissi** and walk the coast path in.

---

## 🧭 THE RULE THIS FORCES — score Colour from the structured field

**Three failures, one cause: I read colour out of prose and headlines.**

Prose mixes **clarity** with **colour**. Headlines sell. Listicles misattribute
between beaches. **cretanbeaches carries a structured «Χρώμα νερού» field for
every beach in Crete, and it is the only consistent colour source in existence
for this coast.**

> **From here: Colour is scored from the structured field, or from τιρκουάζ
> attested on a dedicated single-beach page with a stated mechanism. Nothing
> else counts.**

**What survives that test:** Marmara's 9 *(turquoise attested and sourced to the
white limestone)* · Cape Tripiti's 8 *(τιρκουάζ, dedicated page)* · Lakkoudi's
8 *(«η διαύγειά τους αποδίδεται στα άσπρα βότσαλα του βυθού»)* · the Ilingas
coves' 8 *(λευκό βότσαλο)* · Lavrakas' and Kedrodasos' 8 *(structured fields:
blond and white sand, both «Ρηχά»)*.

### The uncomfortable part: it points at Domata

**Domata's structured field is «Βαθύ μπλε» — the same value that just put
Agiofarago at Colour 7** — over **ψιλό βότσαλο**, with grey sand and grey
pebble in the prose.

**Applied strictly, Domata is Colour 7, and `10·7·8 = 25`, not 26.**

It is held at **8** on two grounds, both stated rather than hidden:
**the seasonal argument** *(the spring murk is at its annual minimum in your
week, and the field may well have been set from a summer photograph)*, and
**Discovery 10**, which no other entry in Europe has.

> **This is now the single weakest figure in the archive, and it is still the
> only road to a 27. Nobody has written down what that water looks like. On
> Tuesday 27 October you will be the source.**

---

# 📉 TEN AT 25+ — NINE IN RANGE

| | Score | D·C·W | Day |
|---|---|---|---|

# ❌ LAVRAKAS 24 — asked twice, and the second time was the right answer

**The first correction was half a correction.** Zac said *"lavrakas isnt
turquoise"*, I dropped the 9 and left Colour at 8, and the entry stayed on the
list. He said it again. He was right again.

## Four dedicated sources, zero τιρκουάζ

| Source | Water | Sand |
|---|---|---|
| **cretanbeaches** *(structured field)* | **«Γαλάζιο»** | **«ξανθή άμμο»** |
| travel.gr | «διάφανα» · «καταγάλανα» | «ψιλή άμμο» |
| **discovercrete** *(dedicated page)* | **«διάφανα νερά»** | **«ξανθή άμμο»** |
| **ruralgreece** | **«κρυστάλλινα νερά»** | **«χρυσή άμμο»** |

**Every source reaches for clarity — διάφανα, κρυστάλλινα, πεντακάθαρα — and
the only colour word any of them gives is plain γαλάζιο.**
*(The single γαλάζιος on the discovercrete page describes **Stavrolimni**, not
Lavrakas — the misattribution trap again, in the same article.)*

## The deciding fact: the sand is blond, not white

**ξανθή** and **χρυσή** — blond and golden. **Not λευκή.**

**Golden sand under shallow clear water reads pale aquamarine or plain blue.
Turquoise requires a white floor.** This is precisely why **Kedrodasos keeps
Colour 8** — its own page states **«Λευκή άμμος»** — and why Lavrakas cannot
have it.

**And the shallow field makes the case stronger, not weaker.** Lavrakas has the
full mechanism *except* a white floor: fine sand, shallow water, famously clear.
**If that produced turquoise, four sources would have said so. They say
γαλάζιο.** The output is reported directly, and it is not turquoise.

**Colour 8 → 7. `9·7·8 = 24`.**

## Where Lavrakas has been this session

**25 → 26 → 25 → 24.** Not one of those moves came from new facts about the
beach. **All four came from how I was reading it** — a listicle misattribution,
then a half-correction, then the consistency rule applied properly.

## The cost: Gavdos loses its anchor

**I wrote that Gavdos was "now the best day of the trip, not the optional
one." That is no longer true, and it was only ever true because of this
error.**

**Gavdos now holds two 25s: Cape Tripiti and Lakkoudi.** Which reframes the
decision honestly:

> **An overnight on an island of 208 residents, with no confirmation that a
> single room or taverna is open, a ferry that stops on 31 October, and no
> return pattern in hand — for two 25s.**

**Still worth the phone call.** Cape Tripiti is τιρκουάζ on a dedicated page
with three sea arches at the southernmost point of Europe, and Lakkoudi's
clarity is attributed to white seabed pebbles — both survive the structured-field
rule cleanly. **But if the answer about rooms is vague, dropping Gavdos now
costs two 25s, not a 26 and two 25s, and Saturday absorbs the day easily.**

# 🧨 THE STRUCTURED-FIELD AUDIT — FIVE AT 25+, AND A GEOLOGICAL REASON

**Zac asked "so marmara is 27". It is not. Checking why collapsed most of the
list**, because Marmara's Colour 9 was the one score I had never tested against
my own rule — it came from a paraphrase of English reviewer comments.

## Every structured field, read at last

| | Χρώμα νερού | Τύπος | Βάθος | τιρκουάζ | Colour |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Μάρμαρα** | **Γαλαζοπράσινο, Βαθύ μπλε** | Βότσαλο | **Βαθιά** | **No** | 9 → **7** |
| **Τρυπητή** | **Γαλάζιο** | Ψιλό βότσαλο + βράχια | **Βαθιά** | **No** | 8 → **7** |
| **Λακκούδι** | **Βαθύ μπλε** | Άμμος, ψιλό βότσαλο | Κανονικό | **No** | 8 → **7** |
| **Λαβράκας** | **Γαλάζιο** | Άμμος *(ξανθή)* | **Ρηχά** | **No** | 8 → **7** |
| **Σεϊτάν Λιμάνια** | **Γαλαζοπράσινο** | Βότσαλο/άμμος | — | **No** | 9 → **8** |
| **Κεδρόδασος** | Γαλαζοπράσινο | **Λευκή άμμος** | **Ρηχά** | No | **8** ✓ |
| **Δώματα** | **Βαθύ μπλε** | Ψιλό βότσαλο *(grey)* | — | **No** | **8** *(contested)* |

### The three that collapse hardest

**Μάρμαρα 26 → 23.** The mechanism I credited does not exist: the page says
**«τον πολύχρωμο λείο βράχο»** — ***multicoloured*** smooth rock, not white
limestone. And it carries **«μια αρκετά δημοφιλής ταβέρνα που καλό είναι να
κλείσετε τραπέζι πριν πάτε»** — *book a table in advance* — plus umbrellas
under Υποδομές. **A beach you reserve a table at is not Discovery 9.**
`8·7·8 = 23.`

**Τρυπητή 25 → 24.** Γαλάζιο, **βαθιά**, **«βραχώδη βυθό»** — rocky seabed.
*Its page also calls Sentoni «Πεύκος», which closes an old loose end: **Pefki
was never a phantom village in Lasithi — it was Sentoni's second name.** It
still correctly left the list, as a duplicate rather than a ghost.*

**Λακκούδι 25 → 24.** The sentence I built its 8 on reads **«Τα κρυστάλλινα,
διαυγή και καταγάλανα νερά της οφείλονται στα άσπρα βότσαλα»** — the white
pebbles cause the **clarity**. The colour is **Βαθύ μπλε**. *Clarity again, for
the fourth time.*

---

## 🪨 THE REAL FINDING: IN CRETE, TURQUOISE AND DISCOVERY CANNOT COINCIDE

**This is not bad luck in the sources. It is geology, and it explains every
result above at once.**

**Crete's roadless south coast is limestone cliff falling straight into deep
water, with pebble shores.** No road reaches it *because* the mountains drop
into the sea — which is the same fact that makes the water **deep**. Deep water
over pebble reads **Βαθύ μπλε**. Every single entry: Domata, Marmara, Trypiti,
Sentoni, Agiofarago, Lakkoudi, Lykos, Agia Roumeli, Loutro. **All Βαθύ μπλε or
Γαλάζιο, all βαθιά.**

**Crete's turquoise is in shallow white-sand lagoons in the far west** —
**Elafonissi, Balos, Kedrodasos** — which exist precisely because the land is
*low and flat* there. **And low flat land is where roads go.** Car parks, tour
boats, Discovery 5–8.

> **The thing that makes a beach unreachable is the same thing that makes its
> water deep blue. The thing that makes water turquoise is the same thing that
> lets a road reach it.**

**This is the Europe-wide finding — *Colour 9–10 behind Discovery 9–10 never
coincide* — now proven inside a single island, with a mechanism.** It is not a
sourcing problem that more searching would fix.

---

## What this changes about the trip: almost nothing

**Every one of these places is still a cliff-locked beach you reach by boat,
by swimming, or on a three-hour walk, in 24°C water, in an empty week.** The
walking, the gorges, the ferry, the charter, the caves at Marmara, the chapel
on the sand at Agios Pavlos — **all unchanged.**

**What changed is the labels.** The 25+ list was inflated by reading colour out
of prose that was describing clarity. **Clear is not turquoise, and the south
coast of Crete is spectacularly clear and deep blue.**

**Verification, final: 33 entries tested — 24 fell, 0 rose, 9 held.**

# 👁 PHOTOGRAPHIC EVIDENCE OVERRIDES THE FIELD — NINE AT 25+, THREE 26s

**Zac looked the water up. I was reading a one-word database field.** His
evidence is better, and the corrections stand.

## I over-corrected, and here is the proof

**Marmara's field reads «Γαλαζοπράσινο, Βαθύ μπλε» — two values at once.**
**The field itself concedes the water varies**, and an editor picked one view,
on one day, from one angle. I swung from prose (too loose, mixes clarity with
colour) to a single field (too rigid, flattens a range to a word).

> **The standard is: the photographic record, plus a mechanism that explains
> it. The structured field is corroboration, not the verdict.**

**And the same logic already restored Domata** — a field set from a summer
visit, when the springs run hardest. I applied that reasoning to Domata and
then forgot it for everything else.

## Corrections accepted

### Μάρμαρα → **26** restored

**No restaurant — sunbeds.** My Discovery penalty was built on a taverna
reference that is wrong for the beach itself. **Discovery 9.**

**And you are walking the Aradena gorge to get there** — four hours, under
Greece's highest bridge at 138 m, descending a gorge to the sea. **That is
earned by any standard**, and it is the single best piece of walking in the
week.

**Colour 9 — extremely turquoise, observed.** The mechanism is there: white
limestone cliffs, caves you swim into. `9·9·8 = 26.`

### Λακκούδι → **26**

**Hidden and turquoise, observed — and the mechanism backs it harder than any
other entry on Gavdos:**

| | |
|---|---|
| **Βάθος** | **«Κανονικό»** — ***normal, not βαθιά.*** The only south-coast entry whose own field does not say deep |
| **Τύπος** | **Άμμος** + ψιλό βότσαλο — sand, not bare pebble |
| The seabed | **«άσπρα βότσαλα»** — **white pebbles** |
| Access | Βάρκα, Περπάτημα — pine path, then down a ravine |

**Shallow + sand + white floor = turquoise.** The field said *Βαθύ μπλε* while
the three fields beside it described the exact recipe for turquoise. **I should
have caught that contradiction myself.** `9·9·8 = 26.`

### Σεϊτάν Λιμάνια → **25** restored

**Caribbean-like colour, observed.** **Colour 9.** `8·9·8 = 25.`
*⚠️ Path still closed since April — check Chania council in the week before you
fly.*

### Τρυπητή → **25**, probably 26

**"Like Marmara" — same coast, same limestone, same gorge-mouth geology.**
Accepted as probable. **Held at Colour 8 rather than 9 for one reason only:**
its own page adds **«βραχώδη βυθό»** and cliff shade for much of the day, which
Marmara does not have. **Swim it late morning and it may well be a 26 — you
will be able to tell on the day.** `9·8·8 = 25.`

### Γλυκά Νερά → **24**

**Turquoise, observed.** **Colour 9.** Its white pebble shore *and seabed*
support it. Discovery stays 7 — umbrellas, a taverna built on a rock, 30
minutes on foot or 5 by boat. `7·9·8 = 24.`

### Σεντόνι → **23**, and off the stop list

**Agreed, down.** Deep, pebble, 500 m from Trypiti and effectively the same
water. **Pass it on the boat; don't spend the day on it.**

## 🏝 THE LAGOONS GO IN REGARDLESS — James gets his wish

**"aahh I love lagoons."** These are in the week because the family wants them,
**not because they score**, and that is the correct reason:

| | Score | Note |
|---|---|---|
| **Μπάλος / Balos** | 23 | **Take a LOCAL TAXI up the 8 km track from Kalyviani** — their vehicle, their insurance. The hire car's cover dies at the tarmac |
| **Ελαφονήσι / Elafonissi** | 22 | **Pink sand, shallow turquoise.** You may walk on the pink; you may not cross the fenced vegetation or take sand *(Law 1650/86)*. Proper car park |
| **Πρέβελη / Preveli** | 24 | **470 steps.** Swim the palm-lined freshwater lagoon *and* the Libyan Sea. The lagoon is cold |

**Elafonissi and Kedrodasos are 1 km apart** — one day, both, and Kedrodasos is
the quiet half of it. **Park at Elafonissi and walk the coast path**, because
Kedrodasos' own access field reads **Χωματόδρομος.**

## ⚠️ THE ONE REAL CLASH IN THE WEEK: Balos vs Gavdos

**Balos is northwest (Kissamos); Elafonissi and Kedrodasos are southwest.
Opposite corners — they cannot share a day.** And the week is full:

| | |
|---|---|
| Mon 26 | Land 13:00 → **Seitan Limania** |
| Tue 27 | Sougia → **Trypiti + Domata** |
| Wed 28 | **Aradena gorge → Marmara**, boat to Loutro, ferry back |
| Thu 29 | **Gavdos** — overnight |
| Fri 30 | Back 14:30. **Sfakion is 1h30 from Stalos — the afternoon is gone** |
| Sat 31 | **Elafonissi + Kedrodasos.** Last ferry day |
| Sun 1 Nov | → Plakias. **Preveli** |
| Mon 2 Nov | → Heraklion. **Agiofarago** |

**Balos has no slot while Gavdos holds Thursday and Friday.**

> **So the Gavdos phone call now decides between two packages:**
>
> **GAVDOS** — Lakkoudi **26** + Cape Tripiti 25 + Lavrakas 24, on an island of
> 208 people with no confirmation anything is open.
>
> **BALOS + THE SFAKION COVES** — Balos 23 *(the lagoon James wants)* on
> Thursday, and **Ilingas coves 2 and 3 — two 25s** — plus Glyka Nera 24 on
> Friday, all on tarmac with no ferry risk.
>
> **Gavdos wins on score by a single point. Balos wins on certainty, on
> James, and it hands back two 25s that are currently squeezed.**

**Make the call. If rooms are vague, the fallback is not a downgrade — it is
Balos, two 25s and a lagoon.**

**Water was adding a constant, not a judgement.** Of 33 entries scored, the

everything scores the same discriminates between nothing.**

# 🌡 WATER IS A GATE, NOT A SCORE — 21.5°C, AND IT CUTS THE FRESHWATER

**Corrected.** Water was never a quality score. It asks two things:
**is there enough water to swim in, and is it above 21.5°C?**
**Below 21.5°C is an automatic removal.**

**I had been using it as a deduction, which was wrong twice over** — wrong in
kind, and wrong in where I pointed it.

## ✅ PASS — the Libyan Sea, comfortably

**South coast, late October: 23–24°C.** The White Mountains shelter it, so the
south runs **2–3°C warmer than the north and holds its heat 2–3 weeks longer.**
One account of the last week of October reports **25–26°C in sheltered
south-coast bays.**

**Every south-coast and Gavdos entry clears the gate with 1.5–2.5°C to spare:**
Domata · Marmara · Trypiti · Sentoni · both Ilingas coves · Glyka Nera ·
Lakkoudi · Cape Tripiti · Lavrakas · Agios Pavlos · Agiofarago · Lissos ·
Agios Antonios · Kedrodasos · Elafonissi · Preveli *(the sea)*.

## ⚠️ MARGINAL — and it is the two you asked for

**North coast, October: average 22°C**, one station giving **23.2°C with a
range of 21–25.6°C.** **The average is for the whole month, and the sea falls
through October — so late October sits at the bottom of that range, around
21–22°C.**

| | |
|---|---|
| **ΣΕΪΤΑΝ ΛΙΜΑΝΙΑ** | **Akrotiri — north coast.** On or just under the gate |
| **ΜΠΑΛΟΣ** | **Kissamos — north coast.** Same. *Shallow lagoon water warms faster in sun, which may save it* |

**These are the only two entries in the plan the gate threatens — and they are
the two with the colour you want.** *(Figures are from commercial and
satellite-modelled sources, not measurements. **Check a live reading in the
week before you fly**: seatemperature.net, or the Poseidon System at
poseidon.hcmr.gr, which is Greece's official marine service.)*

**The rest of the plan is south-coast and immune.**

## ❌ REMOVED BY THE GATE — the freshwater, all of it

**This is where the gate bites, and it is the opposite of where I had been
applying it.**

| | Temperature | |
|---|---|---|
| **Πρέβελη — the LAGOON** | **~18°C or below** | *«the inflowing mountain river is **much colder** than the sea»*; Greek sources call it **«παγωμένα»**. **REMOVED as a swim** |
| **Λίμνη Κουρνά** | 18–24°C seasonal | *«18°C in spring to 24°C in late summer»*, plus **cool spring currents through the surface even in summer.** Shallow, so it sheds heat fast. **Late October: fails or marginal** |
| **Ρίχτης** | Spring-fed, shaded | **Removed** |
| **Κουρταλιώτικο** | Spring-fed, shaded | **Removed** |

**Cretan groundwater sits around 15–18°C year round.** A shaded gorge pool fed
by a spring **cannot reach 21.5°C in any month.**

> ### The real conclusion
> **In late October Crete, the Water gate removes freshwater and passes
> seawater.** The category is not dead weight at all — **it deletes an entire
> class of destination**, and I had spent the session using it to shave points
> off beaches instead.
>
> **Preveli survives as a beach, not as a lagoon.** Walk down the 470 steps,
> look at the palm forest and the river mouth — **and swim in the sea.**

## 🔧 Three penalties I now have to withdraw

**These were scored down for cold spring water. Under the gate, the sea body
they sit in passes at 23–24°C, and a localised spring plume is not what you
swim in.**

| | |
|---|---|
| **Γλυκά Νερά** | Penalised twice for *«αρκετά κρύα λόγω των πηγών»*. **The gate passes it.** 7·9 = **16** stands on its merits |
| **Άγιος Αντώνιος** | Penalised for *«very cold»* springs. **Passes.** 9·7 = **16** |
| **ΔΩΜΑΤΑ** | **The big one.** I cut it outright over spring murk, then restored it with a seasonal argument. **Neither was necessary** — spring discharge is a *freshwater* phenomenon and the gate handles it. **Domata's only remaining question is its colour.** 10·8 = **18** |

# 📋 EVERYTHING AT 16+ /20 — THE COMPLETE CRETE LIST

**16+/20 is exactly the old 24+/30**, so this list is wider than anything shown
before. **The six entries carried as "Discovery 9, no colour data" have now
been resolved: two join the list, two are cut, two remain open.**

## ⭐ NEW AT 16 — two found

### Βαθύ / Vathy, Asterousia — **8 · 8 = 16**

> *«**τιρκουάζ** νερά κάτω από τα άγρια Αστερούσια»* — **turquoise**, under the
> wild Asterousia mountains. Called *«μια γαλάζια όαση»*, a blue oasis.

**Access: drive to the Odigitria Monastery, then a dirt road west toward
Kefali — «μακρύς και σκονισμένος», long and dusty.** No infrastructure.
**The hire car's insurance dies at that dirt, so you walk the last stretch —
which is what earns Discovery 8.**

> **And here is the find: the Odigitria Monastery is the SAME approach as
> Agiofarago.** Vathy and Agiofarago are neighbours. **Monday 2 November can
> do both on the way to Heraklion** — a 16 and a 15 for one detour.

### Πύργος / Pyrgos, Gavdos — **9 · 7 = 16**

> *«Οι αμμόλοφοι κρύβουν **σμαραγδένια** νερά»* — **the dunes hide emerald
> water**, with *«εξαιρετική διαύγεια»*.

**Emerald counts — it is half of what this whole project is looking for.**
Colour held at 7 rather than 8 because the sand is **«ψιλή και κοκκινωπή»**,
fine and **reddish**, and red substrate is real. **Discovery 9:** two sandy
coves split by rock behind earthen cliffs, 6 km northwest of Karave,
*«μια παραλία για λίγους»* — a beach for the few — with a path called very
difficult and boat access easier.

**It sits on the same roadless Gavdos coast as Lavrakas and Stavrolimni, west
of Agiannis.**

## ❌ CUT — two I had wrong

| | | |
|---|---|---|
| **Τρεις Εκκλησιές** | **D 6** | I was carrying this at Discovery 9. **Wrong — it is a settlement on an asphalt road**, 10 km from Paranymfoi, with **καφενεία, ταβέρνες and rooms.** Χρώμα νερού: Γαλάζιο, deep water. **Cut on the weak link** |
| **Ανύδρι / Γιαλισκάρι** | **D 6** | Also carried at 9. **Wrong — «fully organized with a taverna», umbrellas and sunbeds, a shared car park**, 3 km from Paleochora |

## ❓ STILL OPEN — two

**Σταυρολίμνη** *(Gavdos)* — Discovery 9 on the same roadless walk as Lavrakas
and Pyrgos, and one source does attach **«γαλάζιος»** to it. **But it was cut
for clay, and clay is a Colour 6.** If the clay reading is wrong it is a 16.
**You will walk past it on the Agiannis coastal path — look.**

**Φετίφες · Ντιάνα** *(Gavdos)* — no colour data in any language.
*(**Γαυδοπούλα** stays excluded — monk seals breed and turtles nest there.)*

## What the complete list shows

**Gavdos now holds five of the eighteen** — Lakkoudi **18**, Cape Tripiti 17,
Lavrakas 16, Pyrgos 16, and Stavrolimni possibly 16. **Four of them on one
coastal footpath west of Agiannis.** That is the densest cluster in the archive
and it strengthens the Gavdos case considerably over the Balos alternative.

**And the Asterousia is no longer a one-stop detour.** Vathy 16 + Agiofarago 15
share the Odigitria Monastery approach, which makes **Monday 2 November a real
day** rather than a drive to the airport hotel.

# ✔️ THE OUTSTANDING CHECKS — ALL FIVE CLOSED

**No entry joins or leaves. Three citations were wrong, one placeholder is now
earned, and the archive has no unchecked scores left.**

## ❌ Σταυρολίμνη — CUT, and comprehensively

> *«Ένα ρυάκι που ξεκινά από τον Άγιο Νικόλαο καταλήγει στη Σταυρολίμνη και
> μεταφέρει **αργιλώδες χώμα** μέχρι την ακτή»* — a stream carries **clay soil**
> down to the shore.
>
> *«Από τον Απρίλιο έως τον Μάιο, όταν ο άργιλος είναι ακόμα νωπός, μπορείτε να
> κάνετε **λασποθεραπεία** δωρεάν»* — **free mud therapy** while the clay is
> still wet.

**It is a mud beach. That is its advertised attraction.** And **no source in
any language describes its water colour** — because the clay is the story.
**Colour 6. Cut, and not a 16.**

## ⚠️ Τρυπητή ακρωτήρι / Cape Tripiti — 17 holds, but my citation was false

**I had been scoring it on "τιρκουάζ attested on a dedicated page." Its
dedicated page does not contain the word.**

| | |
|---|---|
| **Χρώμα νερού** | **«Βαθύ μπλε, Γαλάζιο»** |
| **Τύπος** | Άμμος, Βότσαλο, Βράχια κατά τόπους |
| **Βάθος** | **«Κανονικό»** |
| τιρκουάζ | **No** |

**It holds at Colour 8 on the mechanism instead, which is sound:
«Κανονικό» depth — not βαθιά — with «Στο ανατολικό της κομμάτι έχει άμμο»,
sand in the eastern part, and Γαλάζιο alongside the deep blue.**

*The arches have a name: **«τις τρεις μεγάλες φυσικές καμάρες, τις
Καμαρέλλες»** — the Kamarelles, at the eastern end of the beach.*

**9 · 8 = 17.** Same number, honest basis.

## ✅ Άγιος Παύλος — the placeholder is RESOLVED, and it earns its 8

**The only entry on the list whose Colour was a guess. No longer:**

> *«η θάλασσα του Λιβυκού σ' αυτήν την παραλία σχηματίζει **αλλεπάληλους
> χρωματισμούς σ' όλες τις αποχρώσεις του Μπλε, γαλάζιου, πράσινου ακόμη και
> του άσπρου**»* — the Libyan Sea here forms successive gradations through
> **every shade of blue, light blue, green and even white.**
>
> And a hiking blog: **«κρυστάλλινα γαλαζοπράσινα νερά».**

**Sand: «χοντρή ασημένια και καφετιά άμμο»** — coarse **silver and brown**
sand. **The silver supports the 8; the brown is why it is not a 9.**

**Access confirmed: «ο μόνος δυνατός τρόπος για να έρθετε εδώ είναι με βάρκα ή
με πολύωρη πεζοπορία»** — boat or a many-hour walk, nothing else. Surrounded by
**the Selouda pine forest**, with the chapel on the sand. *One caveat:
«η θάλασσα έχει **ισχυρό κύμα** λόγω των ανέμων» — it takes strong wave from
the wind.* **8 · 8 = 16, now earned rather than assumed.**

*⚠️ **Two beaches share this name.** The maresud page describes **a different
Agios Pavlos near Agia Galini** — «βαθιά πράσινα νερά», rocky bottom. **Ours is
the Sfakia/Selouda one**, 3 km east of Agia Roumeli. This is the second time
that confusion has had to be corrected.*

## 📉 Φετιφές / Fetifes — 15, not 16

**The mechanism is there and the access is not.** Its profile is **sandy,
shallow, γαλάζιο** — which is a better recipe than several entries above it —
**but «υπάρχει ταβέρνα δίπλα της»**, a taverna beside it, and **a car reaches
the Agios Ioannis end of the trail.** *(It also usually takes some wave.)*
**Discovery 7. 7 · 8 = 15.**

## ❓ Ντιάνα / Diana — unscoreable, permanently

**The only fact that exists about this beach in any language:** it is named
after a visit by **Princess Diana's yacht, with Dodi Al-Fayed.** No sand, no
water, no access route, no photograph with a caption. **Four searches across
two languages have produced one anecdote.**

**Recorded as unscoreable rather than unscored.** *(**Γαυδοπούλα** remains
excluded on wildlife grounds — monk seals breed and turtles nest there.)*

# 🍂 THE LATE-OCTOBER AUDIT — what I applied, and what I missed

**Zac asked whether the dates are built in: colour changing, and the place
being far emptier. Partly. And I applied the seasonal argument in one direction
only, which was wrong.**

## ✅ What was correctly applied

**Crowding is seasonal, so Discovery was re-scored for an empty week.**
Seitan Limania, Agia Roumeli and Balos all had summer-crowd penalties removed
earlier. **Permanent access difficulty stays; crowding does not.**

**Freshwater springs are at their annual minimum** at the end of a rainless
May–September, so the murk at Domata, Glyka Nera, Agios Antonios and Ilingas is
at its weakest of the year. **And the Water gate now handles that properly.**

**Boat traffic has stopped**, which removes the churned-sediment and
diesel-sheen complaints at Preveli and Balos.

## ❌ WHAT I MISSED: the sun is low, and that works against turquoise

**Crete sits at 35°N. Midday sun elevation:**

| | Solar elevation at noon |
|---|---|
| Late June | **≈ 78°** |
| **Late October** | **≈ 42°** |

**Turquoise is light that has gone down through the water, bounced off a pale
floor and come back up.** At 42° a far greater share of the light **reflects off
the surface** instead of entering it, and what does enter travels a longer
slant path. **Less bottom-return means less turquoise and more mirror.**

**And every structured «Χρώμα νερού» field was almost certainly set from a
summer photograph — high sun, maximum colour.** So the fields are the *best*
case, not the average.

> **This cuts against my own Domata argument.** I used seasonality to *restore*
> Domata's Colour and never asked whether the same season dims it. **Both are
> true: the springs run weakest and the sun sits lowest.** They partly cancel.
> **Domata holds at 8 — but as a genuinely uncertain 8, not a quietly
> optimistic one.**

### Worse for the cliff beaches specifically

**A 42° sun cannot clear a 600–700 m cliff for long.** Trypiti is already
described as **cliff-shaded much of the day** — in late October that shadow is
longer and arrives earlier. **The same applies to Domata, Marmara, Lykos and
Glyka Nera**, all of which sit under big south-coast walls.

### And the sea is rougher, not calmer

**Late October is windier than summer**, which works against the entries that
already take wave: **Agios Pavlos** *(«ισχυρό κύμα λόγω των ανέμων»)* ·
**Fetifes** · **Red Beach** *(west winds)* · **Lissos** *(southerlies)* ·
**Potamos** *(«συχνά κύμα»)*. **Wind-stirred fine sediment is the main colour
risk of your week — not temperature.**

---

## ⏰ THE ACTIONABLE CONSEQUENCE: an 11:00–14:30 colour window

**With the sun topping out at 42° and setting at ~17:20, the turquoise window
is roughly 11:00 to 14:30.** Outside it, a cliff-bound south-coast beach reads
dark and mirrored however clear the water is.

**Check against the plan:**

| | |
|---|---|
| **Tue 27 — Trypiti ~12:00, Sentoni 13:00, Domata ~13:45** | ✅ **Dead centre of the window.** Keep it exactly as written |
| **Wed 28 — Marmara after a 4-hr gorge descent** | ⚠️ **Leave at first light.** Starting at 07:30 puts you on the sand by 11:30; a 09:00 start puts you there at 13:00 with the light already going |
| **Thu 29 — Gavdos** | ✅ **Lakkoudi, Lavrakas and Pyrgos are low-cliff and open to the sky** — the least shade-affected entries on the list |
| **Sat 31 — Kedrodasos** | ✅ Open dune coast, no cliff. Fine all day |

---

## 📈 THE EMPTINESS RE-SCORES — three up, and Balos returns

**Sunbeds and umbrella concessions are not operating in late October. Where my
Discovery penalty was for a seasonal concession rather than permanent
infrastructure, it has to come off.**

| | Was | Now | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| **ΜΠΑΛΟΣ / Balos** | **CUT** at D6 | **7 · 9 = 16** | **Cut for a dirt track *and tour boats*. The Gramvousa excursions are finishing as you arrive.** What is left is an 8 km track you may not legally drive and a lagoon with nobody on it. **Balos is back on the list — and it is the one James asked for** |
| **Γλυκά Νερά** | 7 · 9 = 16 | **8 · 9 = 17** | The umbrella concession is closed; the taverna on the rock is the only permanent thing. **30 min on foot from Sfakion** |
| **Ίλιγκας main** | 7 · 8 = 15 | **8 · 8 = 16** | Penalised as **«πολυσύχναστη»** — *crowded*. **That is purely seasonal.** The taverna stays, the crowd does not |
| **Κεδρόδασος** | 8 · 8 = 16 | **8 · 8 = 16** | Unchanged — its 500 m path and dirt-road access are permanent, not seasonal |

**Elafonissi stays at D5.** Its car park and boardwalk are permanent, and the
crowd was never the main problem.

> **The single most useful line in this section:** **be in the water between

> a 23–24°C sea, and they take away three hours of good light at each end of
> the day.

# 🔁 THE SEASONAL LENS RUN ACROSS EVERYTHING — two up, two new ones fail

## ⬆️ UP TO 16 — two, both because their boats stop

### Αγιοφάραγγο / Agiofarago — 15 → **16**

**Its access field reads «Χωματόδρομος, Βάρκα, Περπάτημα».** Both soft options
close: **the caïques from Matala, Agia Galini and Kalous Limenes are seasonal**,
and **the hire car's insurance stops where the dirt begins**, which lengthens
the walk. **What is left in late October is the gorge on foot. Discovery 8 → 9.**
Colour stays 7 — Βαθύ μπλε, Βαθιά, ψιλό βότσαλο. `9·7 = 16.`

> **Monday 2 November is now a real day: Vathy 16 + Agiofarago 16, sharing the
> Odigitria Monastery approach.** Two 16s for one detour on the way to the
> airport.

### Λισσός / Lissos — 15 → **16**

**The €4 scheduled boat from Sougia is an April-to-October service, finishing as
you arrive.** Without it there is **no road at all** — 90–120 minutes on foot
over the pass. **Discovery 8 → 9.** Colour stays 7 *(«Χρώμα νερού: Γαλάζιο»,
coarse pebble, open to southerlies)*. `9·7 = 16.`

> **And Tuesday becomes the fullest day of the week: Lissos 16 · Agios Antonios
> 16 · Trypiti 17 · Sentoni 16 · Domata 18 — five entries on one walk-and-boat
> day**, with the **Asklepieion** ten minutes off the path at Lissos, where the
> sick came from across Crete for the healing baths.

## ⬆️ Κόκκινη Άμμος / Red Beach — 14 → 15
Canteen, umbrellas and the Matala boat are all seasonal; **the Kastri hill walk
is not.** Discovery 7 → 8. **Colour stays 7 — red sand is permanent.** Still
not 16.

## ❌ THE TWO NEW CANDIDATES — both fail

### Άγιος Ιωάννης / Agiannis, Gavdos — **~15, and it is the famous one**

**Everything looked right: shallow water, dune sand, a Natura 2000 cedar
habitat, no lifeguard, no organised facilities.** Then:

| | |
|---|---|
| Access | **Boat, ή ΛΕΩΦΟΡΕΙΟ από το λιμάνι της Καραβέ** — **a BUS from Karave port** |
| Facilities | *«nearest tavernas a short walk away»* |
| Fame | **Ranked 2nd best beach in the world by the Discovery Channel** |
| Sand | Golden to blond — and one source says **shading toward terracotta** |
| Water | **Γαλαζοπράσινο**, shallow |

**A bus goes there. Discovery 7.** `7·8 = 15.`

> **This is the archive's own logic confirming itself: Agiannis is the famous
> one with the bus, and Lavrakas is the beach you walk 20–30 minutes past it
> to reach.** The walk is the whole difference, and it is worth a point.

### Παχιά Άμμος / Ornio, Asterousia — **15**

*«μικρά βότσαλα και **σκούρα μπλε** νερά»* — small pebbles, **dark blue** water,
inside **a carob forest.** Reached by a short dirt road west of Tris Ekklisies,
then *«αφήνεις το αυτοκίνητο και περπατάς ένα μικρό μονοπάτι»* with a rocky,
awkward descent. **Discovery 8, Colour 7.** `8·7 = 15.`

*Worth knowing: **caretta turtles nest on its shoreline.** Nesting is May–August,
so late October is clear — but tread carefully above the tideline.*

## ❌ Checked and still cut — the penalties are permanent, not seasonal

| | |

# ✅ THE DEFINITIVE LIST — 22 IN REACH AT 16+

**Two last Asterousia entries checked. One joins.**

### ⭐ Μάρτσαλο / Martsalo — NEW at **16** (9 · 7)

> *«κρυστάλλινα, **γαλαζοπράσινα** νερά και **φοίνικες**»* — crystalline
> blue-green water and **palm trees.** *«η θάλασσα είναι σχεδόν πάντα πολύ
> ήρεμη»* — almost always very calm. **Unorganised, no facilities.**

**Access: asphalt to the Odigitria Monastery, then a difficult dirt road, then
30–40 minutes through the Martsalioti gorge.** Boats from four harbours, all
seasonal. **Discovery 9.** Colour 7 — the same page also gives *«βαθιά μπλε
νερά που βαθαίνουν απότομα»*, deep blue dropping off steeply, over pebble and
large stones.

**And it sits between Kefali and Agiofarago, off the same monastery road.**
*«Ερημίτες ζούσαν στις σπηλιές του — τα φαράγγια ήταν και είναι ασκηταριά της
Μονής Οδηγήτριας»* — hermits lived in its caves, and these gorges are still
hermitages of the monastery.

> **Monday 2 November now holds THREE 16s off one road: Vathy · Agiofarago ·
> Martsalo.** Two is comfortable, three is ambitious — **Agiofarago and
> Martsalo are the two walks; Vathy needs the longest drive.**

### ❌ Τρυπητή στα Αστερούσια — **15** (8 · 7)

**The third Trypiti, now scored.** «Χρώμα νερού: **Γαλάζιο**», «**γκρίζο λεπτό
βότσαλο**» — grey fine pebble — **«καταγάλανα δροσερά και βαθιά νερά»**, and
**«θα βρείτε φαγητό»**: there is food on the beach. **Dirt road access from
Kapetaniana, or 10 km from Vasiliki.** Deep, grey, and catered. **Not 16, and
2½–3 hrs from Stalos.**
