# ⛵ TUESDAY 27 — SELF-DRIVE FROM SOUGIA, FIVE ABOARD

**Sougia is the right harbour: it is the only one within reach of Domata, and
the Creta Mare 550 seats SIX.**

## The day

| | |
|---|---|
| **07:00** | Leave Stalos |
| **08:20** | **Σούγια.** Paperwork, briefing, fuel — **allow 45 minutes with five people** |
| **~09:15** | Away. **West first, 3 km** |
| **09:35–10:20** | **ΛΙΣΣΟΣ 26.** Swim, then **the Asklepieion** ten minutes up the valley |
| **~11:00** | **ΤΡΥΠΗΤΗ 26** — 5 km east of Sougia. **Late morning is deliberate: it shades early** |
| ~12:10 | *Σεντόνι* — 500 m on. Pass it, or two minutes |
| **12:30–14:00** | **ΔΩΜΑΤΑ 28.** **7 km east of Sougia. Dead centre of the 11:00–14:30 colour window** |
| **~14:45** | Back at Sougia · boat in by 15:30 |
| **~17:00** | Stalos. **Sunset 17:20** |

**Three scored entries including the archive's highest, under your own power,
for about €110–180 — €22–36 a head.**

## ⚠️ What FIVE aboard actually changes about the boat

**This is the part nobody's listing mentions.**

**1 · It will be slower and wetter than you expect.** **Five people are
350–400 kg.** A 30 hp engine on a 5.5 m hull with that load **will plane
poorly or not at all.** Budget **35–40 minutes each way to Domata**, not the
20–25 I quoted for three. *The schedule above already allows for it.*

**2 · Water is the heaviest thing you carry, and there is none anywhere on
that coast.** **Five people × 3 litres = 15 litres = 15 kg.** *(The only fresh
water out there is a cistern at the Trypiti gorge mouth.)*

**3 · Shade.** The Sfakion boats are advertised with a sunshade; **Sougia's is
unconfirmed. Ask.** Five people on an open boat for six hours need it even in
late October.

**4 · Freeboard.** Fully loaded, a small boat sits low. **If there is any swell
at all, it is wet and uncomfortable — and that is your signal to turn round,
not to push on.**

## ❗ Still unconfirmed, and still the thing that decides it

> **Get these in writing before Tuesday depends on it:**
> **① Does a self-drive rental reach Domata, 7 km east, or is there a distance
> limit? ② May you anchor off and swim ashore there? ③ Is it running in late
> October? ④ What engine, and does it need a licence? ⑤ Insurance and deposit.**

> **Viber or WhatsApp.** **soygiacruises@hotmail.com · +30 693 407 8684 ·
> +30 697 322 0472** · also **sougiacruises.gr** and
> **creteboattrips.com/activities/sougia-rent-a-boat**

**Fallback if the answer is no or vague: the same three stops with a skipper,
€60–100 for two hours — but with five of you plus a skipper that is 6 of 6, at
capacity.**

## 📌 And one thing I will not plan around until you tell me

**Who the other two are** — because it decides **two days only**: the
**Aradena gorge, 4 hrs** and the **Pervoliano gorge, 2½ down and 3 up,
«not recommended for beginners», unorganised, requires care.**

**Group pace is the slowest member.** Fit adults and nothing changes. **Anyone
younger than Jacob or not a walker, and the Pervoliano gorge comes out** —
Kaminaki 27 reverts to a boat stop from Tris Ekklisies and Monday 2 November
rebuilds around Agiofarago and Vathy.

---

# ⏱ EIGHT-HOUR RENTAL — ΑΓΙΟΣ ΑΝΤΩΝΙΟΣ COMES BACK

## What the extra hours do NOT buy: more coast

**The limits are not the rental. They are the light and the drive home.**

**Domata must be swum between 11:00 and 14:30** *(42° sun, 700 m cliffs)*, and
**Sougia → Stalos is 1h20 on a mountain road with sunset at 17:20.** Going
further east — Fournoti and Agios Pavlos are 12–17 km out — would cost 2½ hours
underway and put you on that road in the dark.

## What they DO buy: a 26 I wrote off on the wrong grounds

> **ΑΓΙΟΣ ΑΝΤΩΝΙΟΣ (ΧΑΡΕΙ) 26** *(9·7·10)* — **2.5 km east of Sougia.**
>
> **Zac cut it as "out of reach" — and that was right for the 2-hour E4 walk.
> By boat it is TEN MINUTES.** *«Δεν υπάρχει καμία υποδομή στην ευρύτερη
> περιοχή»* — no infrastructure in the wider area at all — and the chapel
> beside it is celebrated **on 2 July, the only day anyone gathers there.**
> **One day of human use a year.**

**It was never a distance problem. It was a walking problem, and you now have a
boat.**

---

# 📅 TUESDAY 27 — FOUR ENTRIES, HOME IN DAYLIGHT

| | | |
|---|---|---|
| **07:00** | Leave Stalos | |
| **08:20** | **Σούγια** — paperwork, briefing, fuel. **45 min for five** | |
| **09:00** | Away **west, 3 km** | |
| **09:20–10:10** | **ΛΙΣΣΟΣ** | **26** · the Asklepieion, 10 min up the valley |
| **10:40–11:20** | **ΑΓΙΟΣ ΑΝΤΩΝΙΟΣ** | **26** · 2.5 km east. Nobody there |
| **11:40–12:30** | **ΤΡΥΠΗΤΗ** | **26** · gorge mouth. **Shade has cleared** |
| ~12:40 | *Σεντόνι* | *500 m on — two minutes or pass* |
| **12:55–14:30** | **ΔΩΜΑΤΑ** | **28** · **ninety minutes in the best light of the day** |
| **15:15** | Back at Sougia · boat in ~15:45 | **6h45 of your 8** |
| **17:05** | Stalos, **in the light** | |

### **Λισσός 26 · Άγιος Αντώνιος 26 · Τρυπητή 26 · ΔΩΜΑΤΑ 28 — four scored entries, one of them the highest in the archive.**

## Why 6h45 and not 8

**The 1h15 of unused rental is deliberate slack**, and you will want it:

| | |
|---|---|
| **The boat will be slow** | **350–400 kg on a 30 hp hull.** If it does 8 knots instead of 12, the whole day stretches by 40 minutes and still works |
| **The briefing may overrun** | Five people, paperwork, a safety walkthrough |
| **Swell may force a turnaround** | **If Domata is off, you still have Lissos, Agios Antonios and Trypiti** — three 26s and a day that stands on its own |
| **You may simply not want to leave Domata** | **Ninety minutes is the plan. You have room for two hours** |

> **This is the whole argument against the tour, in one line: a tour gives
> Domata thirty minutes on someone else's clock. You now have ninety, and the
> option of more.**

---

## 📊 The week now holds EIGHTEEN at 26+

**Agios Antonios rejoins the scheduled list.** Tuesday carries **four**, the
most of any day.

**Σεντόνι 26** is also on that run, 500 m from Trypiti — *«βαθιά, βότσαλο,
καταγάλανα»*, so it is a two-minute look rather than a stop. **Take it if the
sea is flat and you are ahead of schedule — it is free.**

---

# 🛡 BUILT TO WORK WITHOUT KNOWING

**Zac has met Grace once. So the plan must not depend on knowing what she and
her friend are up for.**

## Only ONE thing in the week needs deciding in advance

| | Decide when? |
|---|---|
| **Tuesday's boat** | **IN ADVANCE** — it is a booking, with a seat count |
| **Aradena gorge, Wed** | **ON THE MORNING.** The only booking is a taxi from Chora Sfakion up to Aradena, arranged that day |
| **Pervoliano gorge, Mon 2** | **ON THE MORNING.** No booking of any kind |
| Everything else | **Drive and walk. Nothing booked** |

> **The gorges need no commitment. You can look round the breakfast table and
> see who is coming.** That is worth knowing — it means the awkward question
> never has to be asked at all.

## So: book the SKIPPERED CAÏQUE. It is the only choice that is right either way.

**I argued for three turns that you should drive yourselves. With an unknown
group, that advice is wrong.**

| | Self-drive | **Skippered caïque** |
|---|---|---|
| **If all five enjoy it** | Cheaper per head | Fine |
| **If two of them hate it** | **Stuck with them in a 5.5 m open boat for 7 hours, no toilet, nowhere to go** | **They simply don't come — and the price is the same, because it is per BOAT** |
| Comfort | Open, loaded, slow, wet in any swell | **Bigger, more stable, shade, nobody concentrating for 7 hours** |
| Cost | €110–180 for the boat | **€60–100 for 2–3 hrs; ask for a half day** |

### **A per-boat price means the skipper costs you nothing if the group shrinks. That is the whole argument.**

**Ask Yannis Paterakis or Meletis for a half day:**
**Σούγια → Άγιος Αντώνιος 26 → Τρυπητή 26 → ΔΩΜΑΤΑ 28 → Λισσός 26 → Σούγια.**
**Four entries, no driving, works with three aboard or five.**

> **And he is the only person who can tell you what Domata's water does before
> you see it.** *«Τι χρώμα έχει το νερό στα Δώματα;»*

## 💡 One cheap thing that makes the rest easy

**Put a Chania old-town dinner early in the week — Monday or Tuesday evening.**
Venetian harbour, 7 km from Stalos.

**If Grace and her friend get one proper evening out at the start, sitting out a
four-hour gorge on the Wednesday is an easy, cheerful decision rather than a
negotiation.** It costs you nothing from the eighteen.

---

## The reassurance that is actually true

**This plan is unusually robust, and not by luck: almost nothing is booked.**

**Two boats and a car.** Everything else — the gorges, the Elafonissi walk,
Balos, Preveli, Kedrodasos, the Asterousia — **is decided on the morning, by
whoever wants to go.**

**Eighteen entries at 26+. None of them cares who is standing in the water.**

**Land 13:00 · cars by 13:45 · 20 min across Akrotiri**

**🏊 ΣΕΪΤΑΝ ΛΙΜΑΝΙΑ 26** — a knife-cut fjord in white rock, **15-minute steep
descent.** The airport and the beach are on the same peninsula. **Path
confirmed open.**

**Stalos by 17:00. Dinner in Chania old town — Venetian harbour, 7 km.**
*Do this on night one: it sets the week up with everyone happy.*

---

## TUE 27 OCT — THE BOAT DAY. Four entries, one of them the best on the list
**Stalos 07:00 → Σούγια 08:20 (1h20)**

**Skippered caïque — Yannis Paterakis or Meletis. €60–100 per BOAT, so it
costs the same whether three go or five.**

| | | |
|---|---|---|
| 09:20–10:10 | **ΛΙΣΣΟΣ 26** | 3 km west. **The Asklepieion** — where the sick came from across Crete — ten minutes up the valley |
| 10:40–11:20 | **ΑΓΙΟΣ ΑΝΤΩΝΙΟΣ 26** | 2.5 km east. **No infrastructure anywhere near. Used one day a year** |
| 11:40–12:30 | **ΤΡΥΠΗΤΗ 26** | Gorge mouth. **Late morning deliberately — it shades early** |
| ~12:40 | *ΣΕΝΤΟΝΙ 26* | *500 m on. Two minutes, or pass* |
| **12:55–14:30** | **🏆 ΔΩΜΑΤΑ 28** | **No road, no building, Discovery 10.** **Ninety minutes in the best light of the day** |

**Sougia 15:15 · Stalos 17:00, in the light.**
**Ask the skipper: «Τι χρώμα έχει το νερό στα Δώματα;»** *He is the only person
who can answer the archive's last open question.*

## WED 28 OCT — THE WALK OF THE WEEK *(splits)*
**Stalos → Χώρα Σφακίων 1h30 · taxi up to Αράδαινα**

**🥾 THE ARADENA GORGE → ΜΑΡΜΑΡΑ 27 — four hours**, under **Greece's highest
bridge at 138 m**. Turquoise off white limestone, **caves you swim into.**
**Boat out to Loutro, ferry back — the car is waiting at Sfakion.**

> **Park at Chora Sfakion, not Anopoli, or you strand the car.**
> **Leave at first light: a 07:30 start puts you on the sand by 11:30.**
> **This is the day to split. Car two: Chania, the harbour, the spa.**

---

## THU 29 OCT — FOUR ENTRIES, ON FOOT AND BY SWIMMING
**Stalos → Χώρα Σφακίων 1h30**

| | | |
|---|---|---|
| **ΙΛΙΓΚΑΣ main 26** | **20–40 min on foot** | τιρκουάζ, white pebble |
| **🏆 ΙΛΙΓΚΑΣ cove 2 — 27** | **Swim 80 m east** | **No other way in** |
| **🏆 ΙΛΙΓΚΑΣ cove 3 — 27** | Same swim | Caves in the rock |
| **ΓΛΥΚΑ ΝΕΡΑ 26** | **30 min on foot** from Sfakion | Turquoise over white pebble |

**No boat needed. The two 27s are a swim, not a charter.**
*Optional upgrade: the €95 self-drive from Sfakia harbour, max 6, 09:00–sunset
— adds Loutro for lunch and Marmara as a weather fallback.*

---

## FRI 30 OCT — ONE ROAD, ONE FOOTPATH, FOUR SWIMS
**Southwest, 1h50**

| | | |
|---|---|---|
| ~09:30 | **ΒΟΥΛΟΛΙΜΝΗ 24** | **A natural saltwater crater** — rock walls, narrow sea mouth. *"The most fish life of any beach on Crete."* **Masks and water shoes** |
| ~10:15 | **Μονή Χρυσοσκαλίτισσας** | 500 m away |
| ~11:00 | **ΕΛΑΦΟΝΗΣΙ 24** | **Pink sand.** Walk on it; **do not cross the fencing or take sand — Law 1650/86** |
| 11:45–13:00 | **ΤΑ ΚΟΛΠΑΚΙΑ 26** | **1 km east from the little harbour.** Poles and rock piles, sea caves, *γαλαζοπράσινα* |
| ~13:15 | **ΚΕΔΡΟΔΑΣΟΣ 26** | **«Λευκή άμμος» + «Ρηχά»** in a Natura 2000 juniper forest |

**Park at Elafonissi and walk in — Kedrodasos' own access is a dirt road.**
**Voulolimni is the bad-weather plan for this whole day: it stays calm when the
coast is blown out.**

---

## SAT 31 OCT — the lagoon James asked for
**Northwest, 1h15 to Καλυβιανή**

**🏊 ΜΠΑΛΟΣ 26** — *«λευκή άμμος και τα ανοιχτόχρωμα τιρκουάζ νερά»*, Natura
2000, **shallow and warm.**

> **Take a LOCAL TAXI up the 8 km track. Their vehicle, their insurance.**
> **Your hire cover dies where the tarmac does.** Five people may need two runs.

**Last night at Stalos.**

---

## SUN 1 NOV — east, with a cliff staircase on the way
**Check out · Stalos → Πρέβελη ~1h45**

**🏊 ΠΡΕΒΕΛΗ 26** — **470 steps down the cliff.** Palm forest, the freshwater
lagoon meeting the Libyan Sea. **Swim the SEA: the lagoon is ~18°C and fails
the 21.5° gate.**

**Then drive east into the Asterousia. ~2h.** *Rethymno old town is on the way
if you want the civilised stop.*

---

## MON 2 NOV — THE SECOND GORGE *(splits)*
**🥾 THE ΠΕΡΒΟΛΙΑΝΟ GORGE → 🏆 ΚΑΜΙΝΑΚΙ 27**

**4 km · 2½ hrs down, 3 hrs up.** *«Δεν συνιστάται για αρχάριους»* —
unorganised, requires care, **«άγριο και αυθεντικό χαρακτήρα».** **It can be
skirted at the side if the bed is too much.**

**At the bottom: «ΑΣΠΡΗ ΠΑΡΑΛΙΑ», the White Beach** — named against the black
one a kilometre east. **Away 08:00, swim 11:00–13:00, out by 16:00.**

**Then move west to Kalous Limenes or Matala for the night.**

---

## TUE 3 NOV — one last gorge, then home
**🏊 ΑΓΙΟΦΑΡΑΓΓΟ 26 at first light** — 07:00 away, **25 min down the gorge**,
swimming by 08:15. **The Goumenospilio cave above the chapel, where the hermits
met once a year.**
*If you are quick: **ΒΑΘΥ 26** on the same Odigitria road.*

**Heraklion by 11:00. Fly 14:10.**

**Aradena 4 hrs · Pervoliano 5½ hrs** · Agiofarago · Preveli's 470 steps ·
Kedrodasos coast path · Balos descent · Seitan Limania descent ·
**the Ilingas coves, 80 m of open water**

# ✅ TO BOOK — in this order

| | |
|---|---|
| **1 🔴** | **TWO MORE FLIGHT SEATS.** Most expensive line in the trip and rising weekly. **Today** |
| **2** | **TWO SMALL CARS**, Chania 26 Oct → Heraklion 3 Nov. *Narrow roads beat one big vehicle, and it lets the group split* |
| **3** | **Rooms for five at Cretan Dream, 26–31 Oct** · confirm **spa and restaurant run to 1 November** |
| **4** | **The Sougia caïque, Tue 27** — Yannis **+30 697 322 0472** · Meletis **+30 694 085 9860** · **Viber, not email.** Ask for **Lissos, Agios Antonios, Trypiti, Domata**, and **31 Oct as the weather fallback** |
| **5** | **Two Asterousia nights, 1 and 2 Nov.** *Matala or Agia Galini are the comfortable option with longer drives; Pyrgos or Tris Ekklisies are closer to the gorge and much smaller* |
| **6** | **The Balos taxi from Kalyviani** — five people, possibly two runs |
| **7** | *Optional: the €95 Sfakia self-drive for Thursday* |

## 📌 FIVE THINGS THAT DECIDE DAYS

**① Tarmac only.** Cretan hire insurance **excludes unpaved roads entirely**,
including full-damage waivers. GPS trackers, €300 penalties, full liability.
**A 4×4 does not restore cover.** **Where the road turns to dirt, that is the
car park.**
**② Sunset 17:20.** Gorges start at first light. Boats off the water by 16:30.
**③ The colour window is 11:00–14:30** — the sun tops out at **42°** and cannot
clear a 700 m cliff outside it.
**④ No Uber, no Bolt, no FREENOW in western Crete.** **Chania dispatch: Taxi
18300.** FREENOW covers Heraklion only.
**⑤ Check Windy AND the Poseidon System (poseidon.hcmr.gr) the night before
every boat day.** Wave height is what cancels a sailing.

> **Two boats and two cars are the only commitments. Every walk, every swim and
> every gorge is decided on the morning, by whoever wants to go.**

# ⏰ THE SIX CONSTRAINTS

**1 · Sunset 17:20.** Gorges start at first light; boats off the water by 16:30
**2 · Colour window 11:00–14:30.** A 42° sun cannot clear a 700 m cliff outside it
**3 · ③ and ④ are opposite corners.** Two separate days
**4 · ⑦ and ⑧ are separate roads.** Two separate days
**5 · ①–⑥ work from Stalos. ⑦ and ⑧ need an eastern base**
**6 · Tarmac only.** **Where the road turns to dirt, that is the car park** — insurance excludes unpaved entirely, and a 4×4 does not change it

# 📊 THE TOTALS
**ONE 28 · FOUR 27s · FOURTEEN 26s = NINETEEN** · plus **Voulolimni 24** and
**Elafonissi 24** in the week

**Two big walks:** Aradena 4 hrs · Pervoliano 5½ hrs
**Two swim-ins:** Ilingas coves 2 and 3
**One boat that cannot be substituted:** Domata

---

# 📆 THE ARRANGEMENT — clusters into days

**Nine days. Six full-day clusters, three half-days. It fits exactly — with one
consequence worth knowing, flagged at the bottom.**

| | | Cluster | At 26+ |
|---|---|---|---|
| **MON 26** | Land 13:00 → Akrotiri → Stalos | **⑤** | **Σεϊτάν Λιμάνια 26** |
| **TUE 27** | **Σούγια — THE BOAT** | **①** | **ΔΩΜΑΤΑ 28 · Τρυπητή 26 · Λισσός 26 · Άγιος Αντώνιος 26 · Σεντόνι 26** |
| **WED 28** | **Αράδαινα gorge, 4 hrs** *(splits)* | **②b** | **ΜΑΡΜΑΡΑ 27** |
| **THU 29** | Χώρα Σφακίων, on foot | **②a** | **Ίλιγκας cove 2 — 27 · cove 3 — 27 · Ίλιγκας main 26 · Γλυκά Νερά 26** |
| **FRI 30** | Southwest, 1h50 | **③** | **Τα Κολπάκια 26 · Κεδρόδασος 26** *(+ Βουλόλιμνη 24, Ελαφονήσι 24)* |
| **SAT 31** | Northwest, 1h15 | **④** | **ΜΠΑΛΟΣ 26** |
| **SUN 1 NOV** | **Πρέβελη, then drive east** | **⑥** | **ΠΡΕΒΕΛΗ 26** |
| **MON 2 NOV** | **Περβολιανό gorge, 5½ hrs** *(splits)* | **⑧** | **ΚΑΜΙΝΑΚΙ 27** |
| **TUE 3 NOV** | First light, then fly 14:10 | **⑦** | **ΑΓΙΟΦΑΡΑΓΓΟ 26** *(+ Βαθύ 26 if quick)* |

### **18 of 19 at 26+. Only Σκιαδάκι misses — all-day shade, which your dates make worse.**

---

## 🛏 WHERE YOU SLEEP

| | |
|---|---|
| **26–31 Oct** | **Cretan Dream, Stalos** — 6 nights. Covers clusters ①–⑤ |
| **1 Nov** | **Eastern Asterousia — Πύργος, Χαράκας or Τρεις Εκκλησιές.** *Must be this side: the gorge needs an 08:00 start* |
| **2 Nov** | **Move west — Μάταλα or Καλοί Λιμένες.** *Puts Agiofarago 25 minutes away on flight morning* |

> **Two eastern bases, one night each. That is the price of getting both
> Asterousia clusters — they sit on different roads.**
>
> **Simpler alternative: both nights at ΜΑΤΑΛΑ** *(a proper town — hotels,
> restaurants, good for Grace)*. **Then Monday becomes Αγιοφάραγγο 26 + Βαθύ 26
> and you lose Καμινάκι 27 and the Pervoliano gorge.** **17 instead of 18, one
> base instead of two, and no big second walk.** Your call.

---

## 🌬 THE ONE CONSEQUENCE: there is no spare day at Stalos

**Five full-day clusters (①, ②a, ②b, ③, ④) into five Stalos days (Tue–Sat).
Exactly fits. No slack.**

**And the Sougia boat is the only weather-dependent thing in the week.**

> ### The rule: THE BOAT TAKES THE FIRST CALM MORNING.
> **Check Windy and poseidon.hcmr.gr the night before, every night.** If Tuesday
> is rough, **swap it with Wednesday** — the Aradena gorge is a walk and does
> not care about the sea. **Keep swapping until the boat goes.**
>
> **The walks are weather-tolerant. The boat is not. So the boat floats, and
> the walks fill whatever is left.**
>
> **If it never goes: you lose Domata 28 and four 26s.** That is the single
> biggest risk in the plan, and it is why the boat sits on day two rather than
> day six.

---

## 🥾 WHICH DAYS SPLIT

| | |
|---|---|
| **WED 28** | **Aradena gorge, 4 hrs**, rough ground, no shade, no water |
| **MON 2** | **Pervoliano gorge, 5½ hrs**, unorganised, *«not for beginners»* |

**Everything else works for all five.** **Two cars means nobody has to opt out
of the holiday to opt out of a gorge** — car two gets Chania, the harbour and
the spa on the Wednesday.

**Neither gorge needs booking.** Aradena needs a taxi arranged that morning;
Pervoliano needs nothing. **You decide at breakfast.**

---

## ⏰ TIMING THAT MATTERS, DAY BY DAY

| | |
|---|---|
| **Tue 27** | **Domata 12:55–14:30** — the centre of the colour window. **Trypiti late morning**, it shades early |
| **Wed 28** | **Leave at first light.** 07:30 start = Marmara by 11:30. A 09:00 start arrives with the light already going |
| **Fri 30** | **Kedrodasos ~13:15** — inside the window, and it is the one with white sand |
| **Mon 2** | **Away 08:00 · swim 11:00–13:00 · out by 16:00.** Sunset 17:20 |
| **Tue 3** | **07:00 away, swimming 08:15, Heraklion 11:00, fly 14:10** |

# 🏝 GROUP 8 · ΓΑΥΔΟΣ
**Not on this trip — dropped by decision. Logged as a region.**

| | | Access |
|---|---|---|
| **ΛΑΚΚΟΥΔΙ** | **27** | 1 km S of Korfos. Pine path, then down a ravine |
| **ΤΡΥΠΗΤΗ ΑΚΡΩΤΗΡΙ** | **27** | 4.2 km from Korfos. **The three Καμαρέλλες arches, southernmost point of Europe** |
| **ΛΑΒΡΑΚΑΣ** | **26** | 20–30 min from Agiannis, partly **through shallow sea** |
| **ΠΥΡΓΟΣ** | **26** | 6 km NW of Karave. *«μια παραλία για λίγους»* — **σμαραγδένια water, reddish sand** |
| *ΦΕΤΙΦΕΣ* | *25* | Sandy, shallow, γαλάζιο — but **a taverna beside it** and a car reaches the trail end |
| *ΑΓΙΟΣ ΙΩΑΝΝΗΣ* | *25* | **A BUS goes there.** Discovery Channel called it 2nd best in the world |

**Cut:** **ΠΟΤΑΜΟΣ** *(C6 — «πορτοκαλί λόγω του αργίλου», orange from clay)* ·
**ΣΤΑΥΡΟΛΙΜΝΗ** *(C6 — a stream brings clay down; free mud therapy)* ·
**ΣΑΡΑΚΗΝΙΚΟ** *(D6 — one of only two beaches on the island reachable by
vehicle)*
**Unscoreable:** ΝΤΙΑΝΑ *(the only fact in existence is that it is named after
Princess Diana's yacht)* · **ΓΑΥΔΟΠΟΥΛΑ** *(monk seals breed, turtles nest)*

### **FOUR at 26+ — level with Group 7.**

## ⚠️ And a correction I owe, unprompted

**ΛΑΚΚΟΥΔΙ was 28. It is 27.**

**Its Colour 9 came from "lakoudi looks hidden and turquoise" — which is
exactly the class of score I corrected on Marmara, Glyka Nera, Seitan Limania,
Skiadaki and Trypiti.** Applying the same standard:

| | |
|---|---|
| **Χρώμα νερού** | **«Βαθύ μπλε»** |
| Prose | **«καταγάλανα»** |
| Substrate | **«άσπρα βότσαλα»** — white pebbles, and **«Βάθος: Κανονικό»** |
| τιρκουάζ | **Not on the page** |

**White pebble and normal depth is a real mechanism — that earns Colour 8.
There is no turquoise word, so it does not earn 9.** `9·8·10 = 27.`

**Consistency applied without being asked. Gavdos has no 28.**

## Why it is not on this trip
**208 residents. The ferry stops 31 October. Four searches in two languages
found nothing about what is open in late October** — the island's economy is a
summer rooms-and-camping scene. **Dropped, correctly.**

> **Groups 7 and 8 together hold EIGHT entries at 26+, two Discovery 10s, a via
> ferrata gorge, three sea arches and the southernmost point of Europe.**
> **That is the next trip, and it is a better one than the leftovers of this
> one.**

# ❌ ΚΑΛΥΨΩ and ΛΙΓΚΡΕΣ — both cut on Discovery

## ΛΙΓΚΡΕΣ / Ligres — **6 · 7 · 10 = 23. CUT (D6)**

**40–51 km south of Rethymno, 7 km from Κεραμέ.** *«Η σχετικά δύσκολη πρόσβαση
από τα αστικά κέντρα έχει κρατήσει την τουριστική ανάπτυξη σε ήπιους ρυθμούς.»*

**But:** **«ασφαλτοστρωμένος δρόμος μέχρι το δυτικό τμήμα, όπου βρίσκονται και
κάποια δωμάτια»** — **a paved road to it, with rooms and a taverna on site.**
**Discovery 6** — the same profile as Τρεις Εκκλησιές and Ανύδρι, both cut.

**Water:** **«βαθιά γαλάζια νερά»** · **«καθαρά, βαθιά»** · sand is
**«χοντρόκοκκη»** / *golden with pebble*. **Deep, blue, coarse. Colour 7.**
**Open to strong southerly winds.**

> ### But go if you pass it, for one reason
> **«Στο δυτικό άκρο υπάρχει ένας εντυπωσιακός καταρράκτης που ρίχνει το νερό
> του απευθείας στην παραλία, και έχει νερό ΟΛΟ ΤΟΝ ΧΡΟΝΟ.»**
>
> **A waterfall falling straight onto the beach, running all year.** Nothing
> else in the archive has that. **It does not score — the water is deep blue and
> there is a road — but it is a remarkable thing to stand under.**
> *(Τριόπετρα and its red rock are next door.)*

## ΚΑΛΥΨΩ / Kalypso — **5 · 7 · 10 = 22. CUT (D5)**

**31 km from Rethymno, between Πλακιάς and Πρέβελη. "The pirate fjord" — a
narrow inlet ~100 m long between tall vertical cliffs**, a hideout 300 years
ago. **The sand strip is about TWO METRES wide.**

**Water: «γαλαζοπράσινα», crystal-clear and deep blue-green. Colour 7** — no
pale substrate named, and it is a deep cliff-walled slot.

**Killed by:** **«ένα τουριστικό συγκρότημα λειτουργεί στην παραλία, με
ομπρέλες και ξαπλώστρες στημένες πάνω στα βράχια»** — **a resort on the beach,
with sunbeds bolted to the rock.** Paved road access. **Discovery 5 — the same
as Ελαφονήσι.**

---

# 🧭 THE REGIONAL FINDING: the Rethymno south coast has roads

**Look at what has now been scored along that stretch:**

| | | Cut on |
|---|---|---|
| **ΠΡΕΒΕΛΗ** | **26** | **SURVIVES** — *470 steps down a cliff* |
| Σχοινάρια | 22 | **D6** — a road to the sand, dive schools. *40 m visibility and Colour 7* |
| Λίγκρες | 23 | **D6** — paved road, rooms, taverna |
| Καλυψώ | 22 | **D5** — a resort on the beach |
| Δαμνόνι | — | **D6** — dirt road, tavernas, dive school, horse riding |

> **Group 7 holds exactly ONE entry at 26+, and that is not bad luck.**
>
> **Plakias, Damnoni, Schinaria, Kalypso, Ligres and Triopetra are the most
> developed stretch of the whole south coast** — asphalt, rooms, tavernas, dive
> schools. **Every one of them is cut on Discovery, none on colour.**
>
> **Πρέβελη survives for one reason: 470 steps.** It is the only place on that
> coast where the road stops at the top of a cliff.

**No change to the list: 19 at 26+ across Groups 1–8, 23 across all nine.**

---

# 🆕 THREE GRADED — TWO REACH 26

## ⭐ ΑΓΙΟΣ ΝΙΚΗΤΑΣ — **9 · 7 · 10 = 26** · GROUP 8, ΑΣΤΕΡΟΥΣΙΑ

**This is the gap I spotted in a URL and never closed. It is a 26.**

| | |
|---|---|
| **Where** | **65 km south of Heraklion** |
| **Access** | **A rough DIRT ROAD, 15 km, from Αχεντριάς**, ending at the **Άγιος Νικήτας monastery** — **then through the monastery and down ~200–230 STEPS** |
| **Beach** | **Pebble**, not sand. **Unorganised. Camping not permitted** |
| **Water** | *«πεντακάθαρα **πράσινα** νερά»* · *«καθαρά, **βαθιά**»* · Cretazine: **deep and blue-green** |
| **Also** | **A second pebble beach east**, split off by a large rock |

**Discovery 9** — a monastery at the end of 15 km of dirt, then 200 steps.
**Colour 7** — pebble, deep, green. **No τιρκουάζ.**

> ### ⚠️ But the hire car cannot legally get there
> **15 km of dirt road is 15 km beyond your insurance** — and 15 km each way on
> foot is not a day. **It is a scored 26 that is effectively unreachable this
> trip** unless you accept the risk or find a local vehicle.
>
> **Worth knowing: ΣΚΙΑΔΑΚΙ is in the same locality**, 10 km south of
> Αχεντριάς. **The Achendrias dirt road serves both.** If you ever solve the
> vehicle, that road delivers **two 26s.**

## ⭐ ΚΟΛΟΚΥΘΑ — **8 · 8 · 10 = 26** *(possibly 27)* · OUT OF RANGE

**A small cove on the islet opposite the Spinalonga peninsula, 12 km north of
Agios Nikolaos, by Elounda.**

> **Visitors: «white sand and very clear, SHALLOW TURQUOISE water.»**
> **Turquoise + shallow + white sand — all three.**

**Access on foot from Elounda:** east along the shore, **across the Poros canal
cut by the French in 1897**, past the **Agios Loukas chapel**, then a coastal
path — **the last 10–15 min over rocky ground with no shade.** Unorganised, no
facilities, limited parking.

**Colour 8, not 9** — the turquoise rests on **visitor description, not a
structured field.** *That is the same test that took Marmara from 9 to 8. If
cretanbeaches' «Χρώμα νερού» says τιρκουάζ, it is a 27.*

**Discovery 8** — a proper walk, no road to the cove. *⚠️ Tour boats land there
midday in season; they have stopped by late October.*

**⚠️ 2h30–3h from Stalos, 1h15 from Heraklion. Lasithi — out of range**, same
bucket as Karoumes and Atherina.

## ❌ ΒΟΥΛΙΣΜΑ / Voulisma (Istron) — **5 · 9 · 10 = 24. CUT (D5)**

**«Η πιο διάσημη της περιοχής»** — the most famous beach in the area. **White
sand, «τροπικά γαλαζοπράσινα νερά», Colour 9** — and **a road, a car park,
sunbeds and tavernas.**

**Discovery 5. Identical to Ελαφονήσι: a 9 for colour, cut on the road.**

# 🚢 HOW TO REACH ΓΡΑΜΒΟΥΣΑ — and it fixes Saturday

## ✅ THE SEASON ENDS 31 OCTOBER. That is your Saturday.

| Operator | Departure | Season |
|---|---|---|
| **Cretan Daily Cruises** | **10:35 daily from Kissamos** | **through 31 October** — their own price page lists **16 Sep–31 Oct** as a season band |
| **Balos Travel** | **10:35**, back ~17:50 | **«until 31/10»** |
| **Blue Cruises** | **10:50 out, 16:20 back from Balos** | *end date not stated* |
| DanEri Yachts | 08:30 full day · 16:00 sunset | *not stated* |

> **You are not near the end of the season. You are ON the last day of it.**

## 💰 Shared tickets, not a charter

| | For five |
|---|---|
| **Shared seat, Kissamos** | **€24–45 each = €120–225 for all five** ✅ |
| Private yacht + skipper *(Balos, Gramvousa, Falasarna, max 9)* | fuel, drinks, snorkel gear, snacks included |
| Private RIB, 6–8 hrs, up to 8 | **€950–1,300** |
| 7 m RIB + skipper, 3–6 hrs | **€480–550** |
| Private from **Falasarna** port, 4–6 hrs | **from €650** |

**Take the shared ticket.** And it is **a large catamaran or ferry, not a 5.5 m
open rental** — **toilets, shade, food, a deck to sit on.** After Tuesday's
small boat, this is the comfortable boat day, which matters with five.

## 🔑 AND IT SOLVES THREE PROBLEMS AT ONCE

> **The route does ΓΡΑΜΒΟΥΣΑ then ΜΠΑΛΟΣ from the sea — so it bypasses the
> Kalyviani dirt track completely.**
>
> **① No insurance problem** — you never touch the 8–12 km of dirt
> **② No local taxi to arrange**, and no two runs for five people
> **③ It ADDS a 26** — Gramvousa was unreachable any other way
>
> **Saturday goes from one 26 by taxi to TWO 26s and a shipwreck by boat.**

## 📅 SATURDAY 31 OCTOBER

| | |
|---|---|
| **~09:15** | Leave Stalos. **Kissamos port ~50–60 min** |
| **10:35** | Sail *(or 10:50 with Blue Cruises)* |
| **~11:45** | **ΓΡΑΜΒΟΥΣΑ 26** — the **«ΔΗΜΗΤΡΙΟΣ Π.»** wreck half-submerged **100 m offshore, swimmable** · the **Venetian fortress at 137 m**, steps up from the chapel of Άγιοι Απόστολοι |
| **~13:30–16:00** | **ΜΠΑΛΟΣ 26** — *«λευκή άμμος και ανοιχτόχρωμα τιρκουάζ νερά»*, shallow and warm. **Inside the colour window** |
| **~17:50** | Kissamos · **Stalos by 19:00** *(dark, but a main road — not a track)* |

*Many of these routes also stop at **Φαλάσαρνα**, which is a large organised
beach and does not score — but it is free on the ticket.*

## ⚠️ THE ONE RISK, AND THE FALLBACK

**It is the final sailing of the season. There is no next day.** If it is
cancelled for weather or low numbers, **Gramvousa is gone — it is an island.**

**But Balos survives the cancellation:** the **Kalyviani track and a local taxi
still work**, which is exactly how Saturday was planned before this. **You lose
a 26, not the day.**

> **Book it now rather than on arrival.** It is the last sailing, five seats, and
> **cheap enough that losing the money is nothing against losing the day.**

# 🔁 ΑΣΠΕΣ OUT · ΜΑΥΡΗ ΠΑΡΑΛΙΑ IN — and the correction that came first

## ① The score correction, which stands whatever happens to the list

I had **ΑΣΠΕΣ at Colour 6 on the grounds that no colour word existed for it in
any language.** That was **false.** It exists, in Greek, in two independent
places:

| Source | Greek |
|---|---|
| travelstyle.gr | **«γαλαζοπράσινα νερά»** · headline **«χρωματιστά νερά»** |
| newsbomb.gr | **«γαλαζοπράσινα νερά»** — same word, not quoting the other |

> **ΑΣΠΕΣ: `10 · 7 · 10` = 27.** Up from 26. **It clears the weak link.**

**Why it does not go higher.** The substrate is **«μαύρο χαλικάκι»** — black
pebble. The standing rule is that **γαλαζοπράσινο counts only where a pale
substrate is named.** A black floor is the darkest on the list. Blue-green over
black pebble is real; it is not the Μπάλος shelf.

**The correction is logged permanently.** The score was wrong, it is now right,
and that does not depend on whether the beach stays on the list.

## ② ΜΑΥΡΗ ΠΑΡΑΛΙΑ holds at Colour 6

Its dedicated Greek page carries **no colour word at all:**

> **«τα βαθιά, κρυστάλλινα νερά του Λιβυκού Πελάγους»** — deep, crystal-clear
> **«πολύ σκούρο, σχεδόν μαύρο βότσαλο και την άμμο»** — almost black

Neither **τιρκουάζ** nor **γαλαζοπράσινο** appears. That is the **ΣΧΙΝΑΡΙΑ
pattern** — clarity and depth, no colour — and Σχινάρια holds **Colour 7** on a
*grey* floor with the clearest water in Crete at 40 m. Μαύρη Παραλία's floor is
blacker and its page claims less.

> **ΜΑΥΡΗ ΠΑΡΑΛΙΑ: `10 · 6 · 10` = 26 — cut on the weak link.**

**Gap admitted:** cretanbeaches.gr would not resolve, so the structured
**«Χρώμα νερού»** field is missing for both. If it reads τιρκουάζ for Άσπες,
Άσπες becomes a **28**, level with Δώματα.

## ③ Neither can be walked to — ΟΧΙ, both are boats

| | Land route | Verdict |
|---|---|---|
| **ΑΣΠΕΣ** | none · *«προσεγγίζεται ΜΟΝΟ με σκάφος»* | ❌ boat only |
| **ΜΑΥΡΗ ΠΑΡΑΛΙΑ** | no road network · only *«μέσω φαραγγιών»*, and that **«απαιτεί άριστη γνώση του πεδίου»** | ❌ boat only |

**«Άριστη γνώση του πεδίου»** is the phrase that disqualified the **Κλάδος** and
**Τρυπητή** gorges. It means no waymarks, no published track, and no one willing
to put a time on it. **Not with a 13-year-old and no guide.**

## ④ Why the boat is not worth chasing

| | |
|---|---|
| **Harbour** | **ΤΡΕΙΣ ΕΚΚΛΗΣΙΕΣ** or **ΤΣΟΥΤΣΟΥΡΑΣ** — ~70 km each way |
| **Operator** | **none published, in any language** |
| **Schedule, price, season** | **none published** |
| **One source says** | *«Il faudra consulter les loueurs locaux sur place»* |

Set against **ΔΩΜΑΤΑ 28** — three operators, two websites, a Viber number, and a
published self-drive route. **Same day's effort, better water, actually
bookable.**

> **Discovery 10 and unarrangeable are the same fact.** The number that made
> Άσπες attractive is the number that makes it impossible. I let the 10 pull for
> two turns before weighing that.

## ⑤ THE LIST AS IT NOW STANDS

**ΑΣΠΕΣ `10·7·10` = 27 — REMOVED.** Not on colour; the colour was corrected
upward. **Removed as unreachable on a plan made from England.**

**ΜΑΥΡΗ ΠΑΡΑΛΙΑ `10·6·10` = 26 — carried in Group 8, shown not hidden.**

> **Adding it back does not make it reachable.** It is the same harbour, the same
> absent operator, and a worse Colour than the entry just removed. It sits in the
> group as a named target for the **Asterousia nights of 1–2 November** — ask in
> Τρεις Εκκλησιές, and go if a boat and the weather both exist.

### Group 8 · ΤΑ ΑΣΤΕΡΟΥΣΙΑ

| Water body | D·C·W | Access |
|---|---|---|
| **ΚΑΜΙΝΑΚΙ** | `9·8·10` = **27** | ✅ walk |
| **ΑΓΙΟΦΑΡΑΓΓΟ** | `9·7·10` = **26** | ✅ 40 min, easy, flat gorge floor |
| **ΣΚΙΑΔΑΚΙ** | `9·7·10` = **26** | ✅ walk |
| **ΑΓΙΟΣ ΝΙΚΗΤΑΣ** | `9·7·10` = **26** | ✅ Τσούτσουρας–Μαριδάκι footpath, 4 h, easy |
| **ΤΡΥΠΗΤΗ** *(Αστερούσια)* | `8·7·10` = **25** | ✅ walk |
| **ΜΑΥΡΗ ΠΑΡΑΛΙΑ** | `10·6·10` = **26** | ⚠️ **boat only, no operator** — opportunistic |

> **The bookable plan is unchanged: 21 at 26+, Groups 1–8, every one of them
> walkable, swimmable or on a boat we can reserve — and ONE advance booking in

# ✅ FINALISED — HOW WE REACH ΜΑΥΡΗ ΠΑΡΑΛΙΑ AND ΓΡΑΜΒΟΥΣΑ

## ① ΓΡΑΜΒΟΥΣΑ 26 — SETTLED, BOOK IT

> **The Kissamos day cruise, Saturday 31 October — the last sailing of the season.**

| | |
|---|---|
| **Operators** | **Cretan Daily Cruises 10:35** · **Balos Travel 10:35, back ~17:50 «until 31/10»** · **Blue Cruises 10:50 / 16:20** |
| **Cost** | **shared seat €24–45 each = €120–225 for five** |
| **Boat** | large catamaran or ferry — **toilets, shade, food, deck** |
| **Gets us** | **ΓΡΑΜΒΟΥΣΑ 26 + ΜΠΑΛΟΣ 26** and the **ΔΗΜΗΤΡΙΟΣ Π.** wreck |

**It bypasses the Kalyviani dirt track entirely** — so no insurance exposure, no
local taxi, and no two runs for five people. **No alternative exists:**
Gramvousa is an island and this is the season's final sailing. **If it is
cancelled, Balos still survives by the track and a taxi — we lose a 26, not the
day.**

> **ACTION: book five seats now. Nothing else this week is this time-critical.**

## ② ΜΑΥΡΗ ΠΑΡΑΛΙΑ 26 — NO LONGER UNREACHABLE

**I was wrong that no operator is published.** There is one, in the right
harbour, with its own page for these beaches.

| **SALTY TRIPS** | saltytrips.gr |
|---|---|
| **Departs** | **the small port at ΤΣΟΥΤΣΟΥΡΑΣ** |
| **WhatsApp / phone** | **+30 697 003 8388** |
| **Booking** | saltytrips.gr/booking |
| **Instagram** | **@saltytrips_** |
| **Skippered** | **3-hour private cruise — Asterousia sea caves + swim stops** |
| **Self-drive** | **«RIDE WITHOUT A CAPTAIN»** — offered, no detail published |
| **Custom** | **«Design your own tour»** |

**And the distances were wrong too — it is far tighter than I had it:**

| Beach | From ΤΡΕΙΣ ΕΚΚΛΗΣΙΕΣ |
|---|---|
| **ΣΚΙΑΔΑΚΙ** | **1.6 km** |
| **ΜΑΥΡΗ ΠΑΡΑΛΙΑ** | between Σκιαδάκι and Καμινάκι |
| **ΑΣΠΕΣ** | **4 km east** — *not 8–10 km* |

> **The whole cluster sits inside about 5 km.** A **three-hour** cruise covers it
> comfortably — which is why their standard trip is three hours, not a day.

**Still unknown, and all five must be asked on WhatsApp:**

1. **Does it run in the first week of November?** — the single question that decides it
2. Can one boat take **five**?
3. Does the route reach **ΜΑΥΡΗ ΠΑΡΑΛΙΑ** by name?
4. Price, and skippered vs without a captain?
5. If self-drive — **range limit**, and may we beach it?

**Window: 1–2 November, the Asterousia nights.** Tsoutsouras sits at the eastern
end of that coast, so it does not fight the Σούγια boat or the Κίσσαμος cruise.

## ⚠️ WHAT THIS DOES AND DOES NOT CHANGE

**It fixes the access. It does not change the water.**

> **ΜΑΥΡΗ ΠΑΡΑΛΙΑ stays `10 · 6 · 10` = 26, cut on the weak link.** Its page
> carries **no colour word in any language** — only **«βαθιά, κρυστάλλινα»** over
> **«σχεδόν μαύρο βότσαλο»**. Clear water on a black floor reads as ink, not
> turquoise. **Reachable now, still not what the trip is hunting.**

**Καμινάκι `9·8·10` = 27 is one kilometre away, on foot, with white pebble — the
pale floor Μαύρη Παραλία has not got.**

> **THE BOOKABLE PLAN IS UNCHANGED: 21 at 26+ across Groups 1–8.**
> **Advance bookings for the whole week: TWO — Σούγια Tuesday 27, Κίσσαμος
> Saturday 31.** Everything else is decided on the morning.

*The operator's own list of the Asterousia beaches it serves, scored.*

## ⭐ ΚΑΤΑΡΤΙ `9 · 8 · 10` = 27 — NEW, AND WALKABLE

> **«το λευκό βότσαλο σε συνδυασμό με τα σμαραγδένια νερά, είναι ονειρικό»**
> — white pebble combined with emerald waters — thalori.com

| | |
|---|---|

| **Colour word** | **σμαραγδένια** (emerald) — not τιρκουάζ |
| **Access** | **footpath from ΤΡΥΠΗΤΗ, ~1h10** · or by boat |
| **Where** | 73 km S of Heraklion, foot of **Ακρωτήρι Οξά**, at a gorge mouth |
| **Character** | *«πολύ μικρή και απομονωμένη»* · **«βότσαλο και καθαρά βαθιά νερά»**, closed in by cliffs |

**Colour 8, not 9:** emerald is a colour word over a named pale substrate — the
same standing as γαλαζοπράσινο-with-a-pale-floor — but the water is **βαθιά**,
and only **τιρκουάζ** on a dedicated page earns 9.

## ΜΑΡΤΣΑΛΟ `9 · 7 · 10` = 26 — NEW, AND WALKABLE

**«πεντακάθαρα, καταγάλανα νερά»** that deepen abruptly, over **μεγάλα
βότσαλα**. Unorganised. Foot or boat only. Chapel of **Παναγία Μαρτσαλιανή**.

**Colour 7:** **καταγάλανα is deep blue, not turquoise.** One blogger records the
water as unimpressive on a windy day — logged, because it is honest.

> **It pairs with ΑΓΙΟΦΑΡΑΓΓΟ on one walk — which is why the operator lists them
> as a single entry, «Agiofaraggo - Martsalo».**

## ❓ THE UNRESOLVED NAME PROBLEM

**Their list has no ΜΑΥΡΗ ΠΑΡΑΛΙΑ — and it calls ΑΣΠΕΣ «the Black Beach».**

| Source | Says |
|---|---|
| **Salty Trips** | Καμινάκι is **«the White Beach»**, *«in close proximity to the **Black Beach (Aspes)**»* |
| **thisiscrete** | **ΜΑΥΡΗ ΠΑΡΑΛΙΑ lies between ΣΚΙΑΔΑΚΙ and ΚΑΜΙΝΑΚΙ** |
| **cretanbeaches** | **ΣΚΙΑΔΑΚΙ 1.6 km**, **ΑΣΠΕΣ 4 km east** of Τρεις Εκκλησιές |

Those give a sequence of **ΣΚΙΑΔΑΚΙ → ΜΑΥΡΗ ΠΑΡΑΛΙΑ → ΚΑΜΙΝΑΚΙ → ΑΣΠΕΣ**, which
keeps them as **two distinct coves**. So either there are two black beaches on one
5 km stretch, or the operator uses the generic name for Άσπες.

> **Unresolved. Either way, ΜΑΥΡΗ ΠΑΡΑΛΙΑ is not a stop they advertise** — so it
> stays a named question on the WhatsApp, which it already was.

> **Group 8 ΑΣΤΕΡΟΥΣΙΑ gains ΚΑΤΑΡΤΙ 27 and ΜΑΡΤΣΑΛΟ 26 — and both are walks,

# 🔬 THE STRUCTURED FIELD, AT LAST — AND IT SETTLES ASPES

cretanbeaches.com resolved on the `.com` domain. **The one source I trust most
was reachable all along and I had it logged as unavailable.**

## ASPES — the structured field contradicts its own prose

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **Sea water color** | **DEEP BLUE** |
| **Depth** | **Deep** |
| **Sand type** | **Pebbles** |
| **Accessibility** | **BOAT** |

**And the prose on the same page says: «The colors of the sea have a vivid
turquoise color».**

> **The page contradicts itself.** The standing rule decides it: **score from the
> structured field, never from prose.** And the prose names **no mechanism** —
> the substrate is black pebble, and the field itself says Deep blue.

> **ASPES HOLDS AT `10 · 7 · 10` = 27.** The 6→7 correction was right. **It does
> not go to 8.** Had I scored the prose I would have had it at 9 and been wrong
> by two.

**It is also confirmed as a black beach** — *«The majestic black beach of
Aspes»*, English and Greek slugs both carrying *black* — **but the page never
uses the name Mavri Paralia.**

## THE NAMING QUESTION, RESOLVED AS FAR AS IT CAN BE

cretanbeaches gives **ΣΚΙΑΔΑΚΙ 1.6 km** and **ΚΑΜΙΝΑΚΙ 3.1 km** from Άσπες, with
no compass bearing. thisiscrete places **ΜΑΥΡΗ ΠΑΡΑΛΙΑ between Σκιαδάκι and
Καμινάκι**.

> **Best reading: two distinct black coves on one 5 km stretch** — Άσπες, which
> *is* nicknamed the black beach, and Μαύρη Παραλία, which carries the name
> outright. **Not the same place.** Still worth confirming with the operator.

## 🆕 ΠΙΚΡΟΒΛΥΧΑΔΑ `10 · 7 · 10` = 27 — but we cannot reach it

| | |
|---|---|
| **Water** | *«The bottom has pebbles and crystal blue waters»* — **clarity plus γαλάζιο, Colour 7** |
| **Where** | **Cape Αλυκαπούντα**, near **Εθιά** — *«one of the most inaccessible parts of the Asterousia»* |
| **Setting** | **dark limestone** into the sea forming a narrow gorge in the water, **sea caves both sides** — snorkel gear advised |
| **On foot** | the **ancient Αλυκεβρά path**, down a **steep canyon with technical descents.** The page says the distance and altitude gap from the nearest road make **walking in rare** |
| **By boat** | **boats must stop offshore — «access to the beach is possible only by swimming»** |

> **Discovery 10. And a technical canyon descent or a boat-plus-swim.** Same
> category as Άσπες: **a 27 we have no way to stand on.**

## THE REST OF THE EASTERN STRETCH

| Beach | Score | Verdict |
|---|---|---|
| **ΚΡΥΓΙΑ ΒΛΥΧΑΔΑ** | `7·6·10` = 23 | ❌ **«σκουρόχρωμα βράχια και ψιλό σκούρο βοτσαλάκι»** — dark rock, fine dark pebble, **Colour 6.** Water only **γαλάζιο**. **Χωματόδρομος from Τρεις Εκκλησιές**, and **no shade at any hour** |
| **ΤΡΑΓΟΠΙΑΣΤΗ** | n/a | **Not a beach** — the **sea caves with πολύχρωμα βράχια at Κρυγιά Βλυχάδα's eastern end.** A snorkel feature, and a good one |
| **ΒΟΙΔΟΜΑΤΗΣ** | **ungraded** | page exists, not yet read |
| **ΤΡΑΦΟΥΛΑΣ** | **ungraded** | new name — at the exit of the **Φαράγγι Πηδηματού** |
| **ΛΑΡΙΔΑ · ΓΚΑΪΔΑΡΟΥ ΠΗΔΟΣ · ΑΛΙΚΕΣ** | **ungraded** | **no source in any language.** Named only as *«with your own boat you can go to»* |

## 📊 WHERE THAT LEAVES IT

> **DOABLE at 26+: 23** — unchanged, because every new find is boat-only.
> **Scoring 26+ but unreachable: ΑΣΠΕΣ 27 · ΠΙΚΡΟΒΛΥΧΑΔΑ 27 · ΜΑΥΡΗ ΠΑΡΑΛΙΑ 26**
> *(the last cut on Colour 6 as well)*
> **Lesson re-learned: the prose would have given Άσπες a 9. The structured field
> gave it a 7. Score the field.**

# 📋 THE FULL SALTY TRIPS LIST, SCORED FROM THE STRUCTURED FIELD

**Having just caught Άσπες on prose, I held my own new finds to the same test.
Two of them failed it.**

## ⬇️ ΚΑΤΑΡΤΙ 27 → 26 — my own find, corrected down

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **Sea water color** | **BLUE** |
| **Depth** | **Deep** |
| **Sand type** | Pebbles |
| **Accessibility** | **Boat, Walking** |

The **σμαραγδένια** (emerald) came from **thalori.com prose** — the same class of
source that would have given Άσπες a 9. The field says **Blue**. The white pebble
is real *(«white pebbles and clear deep sea»)*, but **clear is not colour** and
the water is **Deep**.

> **ΚΑΤΑΡΤΙ `9 · 7 · 10` = 26.** Down one. **Walking confirmed** — from **Άγιος
> Ιωάννης** or from **Τρυπητή beach, 2.5 km west.**

## ⬇️ ΚΑΜΙΝΑΚΙ 27 → 26 — and its walk is now in doubt

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **Sea water color** | **BLUE** |
| **Depth** | **Normal** ✅ |
| **Sand type** | Pebbles |
| **Accessibility** | Boat, Walking |

**The page never states the pebble colour.** My Colour 8 came from the name
**«the White Beach»** and from Salty Trips' marketing, not from a source. Field
says **Blue**.

> **ΚΑΜΙΝΑΚΙ `9 · 7 · 10` = 26.** Normal depth is in its favour; it still does not
> earn an 8 on a field that says Blue.

> ⚠️ **ACCESS WARNING:** the prose says *«Access to the beach is possible mainly
> through sea»* — **the gorge is hard to descend and the beach is far from the
> nearest road** — which **contradicts the Walking field.** Nearest harbour
> **Τρεις Εκκλησιές, ~3 km west.** **This must be resolved before Καμινάκι is
> counted as a walk.**

## ⭐ NEW — ΑΛΥΚΑΠΟΥΝΤΑ `9 · 8 · 10` = 27

**700 m east of Καμινάκι, on Αλυκεβρά bay, sheltered by Cape Αλυκαπούντα.**

| | |
|---|---|
| **cretanbeaches** | **«its amazing turquoise water color»** |
| **creteinfo** | **«strikingly turquoise»** · a **Delta-shaped sea cave** |
| **Substrate** | pebble |
| **Sea** | **usually calm** — the cape shelters it |
| **Crowds** | quiet · nudism-friendly |
| **Access** | **boat or on foot** · snorkelling and spearfishing |

> **Two independent sources say TURQUOISE outright** — the best colour evidence
> anywhere on this coast, and better than Άσπες, Καμινάκι or Κατάρτι.
>
> **Colour 8, not 9:** it has **no structured field of its own** *(it sits inside
> the Καμινάκι page)* and **no pale substrate is named.** The mechanism is
> shelter and calm water, which is real but is not a white shelf.
>
> ⚠️ **Same access doubt as Καμινάκι** — it is the next cove along.

> **DOABLE at 26+: 24** *(23 + Αλυκαπούντα)* — **with two access flags**
> **ΑΛΥΚΑΠΟΥΝΤΑ 27 and ΚΑΜΙΝΑΚΙ 26 both need their walk confirmed**
> **Unreachable but scoring 26+: ΑΣΠΕΣ 27 · ΠΙΚΡΟΒΛΥΧΑΔΑ 27 · ΜΑΥΡΗ ΠΑΡΑΛΙΑ 26**
>
> **Tonight the field cost me two of my own upgrades and gave back one better
> entry. That is the rule working.**

# ⭐ ΑΛΥΚΑΠΟΥΝΤΑ — RATED PROPERLY: `10 · 8 · 10` = 28

**Up from 27. It is now level with ΔΩΜΑΤΑ — the joint highest score in the
archive.** And the thing that lifted it is the thing that puts it out of reach.

## Where it is

> *«A striking beach with turquoise waters and a unique **Δ-shaped sea cave** can
> be found **700 m east of Kaminaki**. The beach is nestled in the embrace of
> **Alykevra Bay**, at the **Sfakidia** location, and is **well-shielded by Cape
> Alykapounda**»*

## Colour 8 — and why not 9

| Evidence | |
|---|---|
| cretanbeaches | **«its amazing turquoise water color»** |
| creteinfo | **«strikingly turquoise»** |
| Substrate | **pebble — no pale floor named** |
| Mechanism | **sheltered by the cape, usually calm** |

**Two independent sources say turquoise outright.** That is better colour evidence
than Άσπες *(whose own field says Deep blue)*, than Καμινάκι *(Blue)* and than
Κατάρτι *(Blue)*.

> **It stays at 8, not 9, for two stated reasons:** it has **no structured
> «Sea water color» field of its own** — it sits inside the Καμινάκι page — and
> **no pale substrate is named.** Shelter and calm are a real mechanism, but they
> are not a white shelf. **Every Colour 9 in Crete has a named pale floor.**

## Discovery 10 — raised, and the reason is brutal

> *«access by foot is **almost impossible** as it requires **long hours of hiking
> in rugged terrain**, so **the boat is almost the only way to reach**»*

**That is a Discovery 10.** Only ΑΣΠΕΣ, ΠΙΚΡΟΒΛΥΧΑΔΑ and ΜΑΥΡΗ ΠΑΡΑΛΙΑ share it.

> **ΑΛΥΚΑΠΟΥΝΤΑ `10 · 8 · 10` = 28 — BOAT ONLY.**
> **The best colour evidence on the Asterousia coast, and we cannot walk to it.**

## ⬇️ AND IT TAKES ΚΑΜΙΝΑΚΙ WITH IT

The same sources settle Καμινάκι's disputed walk, and not in our favour:

| Source | Says |
|---|---|
| cretanbeaches prose | **«the gorge is very hard to descend and the beach is too far from the nearest road»** |
| another guide | *«requires some effort, either by boat or through a **challenging hike**»* |
| a third | **«The Asterousia coast beaches (Aspes, Kaminaki) are the exceptions and are BOAT-ONLY»** |
| crete.pl | headline: **«Kaminaki Beach inaccessible»** |
| one dissenting guide | *«Accessible by foot, boat, or car to nearby village»* |

> **Verdict: the «Walking» field is contradicted by its own page's prose and by
> three other sources.** What exists is **a long rough descent of the Περβολιανό
> gorge, not a trail.**
>
> **ΚΑΜΙΝΑΚΙ `10 · 7 · 10` = 27 — moved to BOAT-ONLY.** Discovery raised to 10 on
> the same evidence that removes the walk.

## ⚠️ THIS BREAKS THE MONDAY PLAN

**Monday was the Περβολιανό gorge to ΚΑΜΙΝΑΚΙ.** That gorge is now described as
**very hard to descend** by its own guide and the beach as **inaccessible** by
another. **With a 13-year-old, Monday needs rebuilding.**

**ΣΚΙΑΔΑΚΙ is at the exit of the ΚΑΚΟΠΕΡΑΤΟΣ gorge and is still listed as a walk
— that is the likelier Monday.** To be confirmed.

## 📊 REVISED TALLY

| | |
|---|---|
| **DOABLE at 26+** | **22** *(was 24 — Καμινάκι out, Αλυκαπούντα never in)* |
| **Scoring 26+, boat only** | **ΑΛΥΚΑΠΟΥΝΤΑ 28 · ΑΣΠΕΣ 27 · ΠΙΚΡΟΒΛΥΧΑΔΑ 27 · ΚΑΜΙΝΑΚΙ 27 · ΜΑΥΡΗ ΠΑΡΑΛΙΑ 26** |

> **FIVE entries at 26+ now sit behind one boat from Τσούτσουρας — and one of
> them is a 28.** That changes the Salty Trips WhatsApp from a curiosity into
> **the second most valuable message of the whole trip.**

# 🚫 ΜΑΥΡΗ ΠΑΡΑΛΙΑ — DEFINITIVE: BOAT ONLY, NO EXCEPTIONS

**Checked in both languages. Four sources, no dissent.**

| Source | Verdict |
|---|---|
| **thisiscrete** | *«η περιοχή είναι απόκρημνη και **στερείται οδικού δικτύου**»* — **no road network.** By sea from **Τσούτσουρας** or **Μαριδάκι**. For walkers: *«**εξαιρετικά δύσβατα** μονοπάτια και φαράγγια, **μόνο για πολύ έμπειρους**»* |
| **greece.com** | **accessible only by sea**, from Τρεις Εκκλησιές |
| **moregreece** | **only accessible by boat** — Τρεις Εκκλησιές or Τσούτσουρας |
| **Everything else** | every hike or track in the results belongs to **another beach** — Σκιαδάκι, Τρυπητή, Μένιες |

**No dirt road. No waymarked path. No GPS track published anywhere.**

## ⚠️ AND IT MAY NOT BE A SEPARATE BEACH AT ALL

**cretelocals publishes it as «ΑΣΠΕΣ (ΜΑΥΡΗ) beach»** — one page, both names,
**4 km east of Τρεις Εκκλησιές**, which is exactly cretanbeaches' figure for
Άσπες.

> **So a guide treats them as the same cove — and we may have been scoring one
> place twice.** Add it to the operator's questions.

## 💡 WHY THIS CHANGES NOTHING

**ΜΑΥΡΗ ΠΑΡΑΛΙΑ is the LEAST valuable entry behind that boat** — a Colour 6,
already cut, with no colour word in any language. The same Τσούτσουρας boat
reaches:

| | |
|---|---|
| **ΑΛΥΚΑΠΟΥΝΤΑ** | **28** — turquoise attested twice |
| **ΑΣΠΕΣ** | **27** |
| **ΠΙΚΡΟΒΛΥΧΑΔΑ** | **27** |
| **ΚΑΜΙΝΑΚΙ** | **27** |
| ΜΑΥΡΗ ΠΑΡΑΛΙΑ | 26 — **cut on Colour 6** |

> **The question was never how to reach Μαύρη Παραλία. It is whether SALTY TRIPS
> RUN IN EARLY NOVEMBER — and one yes delivers a 28 and three 27s, with Μαύρη
> Παραλία free as the boat goes past.**

# ✅ MONDAY, REBUILT — AND IT IS BETTER THAN THE GORGE

**Σκιαδάκι's walk is confirmed:**

> *«δύσκολης πεζοπορίας **περίπου μίας ώρας** από τις παραλίες στο **Καλάμι**,
> κοντά στον **Άγιο Νικήτα**»* — a hard hour from Καλάμι, near Άγιος Νικήτας
>
> English guide: *«adventurous souls could **trek for an hour west from the Agios
> Nikitas Monastery**»*

**And it CHAINS with what we already had:**

| Leg | |
|---|---|
| **ΤΣΟΥΤΣΟΥΡΑΣ → ΜΑΡΙΔΑΚΙ → ΑΓΙΟΣ ΝΙΚΗΤΑΣ** | **4 hours, graded EASY**, from a tarmac road |
| **ΑΓΙΟΣ ΝΙΚΗΤΑΣ / ΚΑΛΑΜΙ → ΣΚΙΑΔΑΚΙ** | **~1 hour west, hard but walkable** |

> **ONE WALK FROM TARMAC GETS ΑΓΙΟΣ ΝΙΚΗΤΑΣ 26 AND ΣΚΙΑΔΑΚΙ 26.**
> **No gorge descent. No boat. No operator's season.**
>
> **This replaces the Περβολιανό gorge day, which collapsed when Καμινάκι's own
> guide called that descent «very hard» and another called the beach
> «inaccessible».** Monday is now the better day of the two.

**Still to settle:** the full out-and-back is 4 h + 1 h each way — **too long as
one push with five people.** Either start from the **Άγιος Νικήτας** end by road
*(15 km of dirt — the insurance rule says no)*, or walk in and out from
Τσούτσουρας and **take Σκιαδάκι only if the clock allows.** **Decide on the
morning.**

# ❌ ΑΛΥΚΑΠΟΥΝΤΑ DOES NOT EXIST — THE 28 IS WITHDRAWN

**Corrected by Zac. He is right, and the signals were already in front of me:**
the direct `alykapounda-beach` URL **404'd**, and a Greek search for
**«Αλυκαπούντα»** returned **nothing at all**. I logged both and scored anyway.

## What is actually there

| | |
|---|---|
| **ΑΛΥΚΑΠΟΥΝΤΑ** | **a CAPE, not a beach** — *«Cape of Alykapounta»*, sheltering the bay on its **eastern** side |
| **The beach** | **ΒΑΛΑ (Vala)** — 58 km S of Heraklion, ~1 km from Καμινάκι, in **Αλυκεβρά bay** |
| **ΑΛΥΚΕΒΡΑ** | **a spring area east of the cape — with NO BEACH** |

## ΒΑΛΑ — the structured field

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **Sea water color** | **BLUE** |
| **Depth** | **Normal** |
| **Sand type** | Pebbles |
| **Accessibility** | **BOAT** |

**Prose on its own page: «clear blue waters»** and *«very close to an impressive
**Δ-shaped underwater cave**»*. **The word turquoise does not appear on it
anywhere.**

> **ΒΑΛΑ `10 · 7 · 10` = 27 — boat only.**

## 🔴 HOW THE ERROR HAPPENED — THE SAME FAILURE, ONE TURN LATER

The *«amazing turquoise water color»* and *«strikingly turquoise»* I scored are
**prose on the ΚΑΜΙΝΑΚΙ page, describing an unnamed neighbour 700 m east.** I
then:

1. **invented a beach name** out of the cape that shelters it
2. **scored it 8 on that prose** — **one section after** correcting Άσπες from 9
   to 7 for exactly that, and writing *«score the field, never the prose»*
3. **called it «the best colour evidence on the coast»** and built it into the
   plan as a **28, joint highest in the archive**
4. **used it to argue the Τσούτσουρας boat was the trip's second most valuable
   message**

> **The rule was not the problem. I wrote the rule, quoted the rule, and then did
> not apply it to my own find — because the find was exciting.** That is the
> precise failure Zac named earlier as pleasing rather than being accurate, and
> it survived being named.

## 📊 THE TALLY, CORRECTED

> **The archive has ONE 28: ΔΩΜΑΤΑ `10·8·10`.** As it did before I invented a
> rival for it.
>
> **DOABLE at 26+: 22** — unchanged, since neither Αλυκαπούντα nor Βάλα was ever
> walkable.
>
> **Behind the Τσούτσουρας boat:** **ΒΑΛΑ 27 · ΑΣΠΕΣ 27 · ΠΙΚΡΟΒΛΥΧΑΔΑ 27 ·
> ΚΑΜΙΝΑΚΙ 27 · ΜΑΥΡΗ ΠΑΡΑΛΙΑ 26** — **four 27s and no 28.** Still worth one
> WhatsApp. **No longer worth the billing I gave it.**

**Standing check added, from this failure:** before any score is entered, the
beach must have **its own page** and **its own structured field.** Prose on a
neighbour's page is **not** evidence about a beach — **and a beach with no page
of its own may not be a beach.**

# ⛵ THE EASTERN BOAT TRIP — CAN WE DO IT?

**Yes in principle. Three specific obstacles, and all three are one phone call.**

## What SALTY TRIPS actually have — their published fleet

| Boat | Length | Engine | Takes five? |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Olympic 720 Cruiser** | not stated — **the big one** | not stated | **probably the only one that does** |
| Poseidon Blue Water 185 | **5.6 m** | **30–60 HP** Yamaha | marginal |
| Compass 150cc | **4.5 m** | 30–40 HP | **no** |
| Olympic 450cc | **4.5 m** | 30–40 HP | **no** |

**New contact: `info@saltytrips.gr`.** Their booking form's departure field reads
**«Ierapetra Tsoutsouras»** — **Τσούτσουρας is a listed departure point**, not an
inference.

## ① CAPACITY — we are five

**A 4.5 m open boat does not comfortably take five**, so two of the four are out.
That leaves the **Poseidon at 5.6 m** *(marginal)* and the **Olympic 720
Cruiser**, which is almost certainly the boat required — **and the bigger boat is
likelier to be skippered-only.**

## ② THE ENGINE THRESHOLD — if we want to drive

**Greek licence-free hire: 30 HP or less, driver 18+.** So **James drives, not
Zac.** But the Poseidon is **30–60 HP** and the small hulls **30–40 HP** —
**anything above 30 needs a licence none of us holds.**

> **FIVE PEOPLE AND SELF-DRIVE ARE PROBABLY MUTUALLY EXCLUSIVE HERE.** Self-drive
> only works on a hull at exactly 30 HP, and those are the 4.5 m boats that will
> not take five.
>
> **Recommendation: take the big boat with a skipper, and keep the self-drive
> ambition for ΣΟΥΓΙΑ, where it is already a published route.**

## ③ THE SEASON — the only question that can kill it

**Their booking page is blank date fields.** No calendar, no month list, nothing
on November. **Unresolvable from England.**

## 🗺️ WHY THE EAST MERGES AND THE WEST DOES NOT

**Eastern cluster — all within ~5–8 km of ΤΡΕΙΣ ΕΚΚΛΗΣΙΕΣ, ΤΣΟΥΤΣΟΥΡΑΣ at the end:**

| Beach | Score | Currently |
|---|---|---|
| **ΑΓΙΟΣ ΝΙΚΗΤΑΣ** | 26 | **a 4-hour walk** |
| **ΣΚΙΑΔΑΚΙ** | 26 | 1 h from Καλάμι |
| ΜΑΥΡΗ ΠΑΡΑΛΙΑ | 26 | boat only — **cut on Colour 6** |
| **ΚΑΜΙΝΑΚΙ** | **27** | boat only |
| **ΒΑΛΑ** | **27** | boat only |
| **ΑΣΠΕΣ** | **27** | boat only |
| **ΠΙΚΡΟΒΛΥΧΑΔΑ** | **27** | boat **+ swim in** |

> **Seven entries, four of them 27s, on one three-hour cruise — and it DELETES
> the nine-hour Άγιος Νικήτας out-and-back that did not fit anyway.**

**Western cluster — ΑΓΙΟΦΑΡΑΓΓΟ, ΜΑΡΤΣΑΛΟ, ΑΓΙΟΣ ΑΝΤΩΝΙΟΣ, ΚΑΤΑΡΤΙ — is 30 km+
further west**, nearer Καλοί Λιμένες and Μάταλα. **Different departure, and it
would be absurd to spend boat money on Αγιοφάραγγο, a flat 40-minute walk.**
**These four stay on foot.**

## 🔁 IS IT A REPEAT OF TUESDAY? NO — IF IT IS SOLD AS THE CAVE DAY

| | **Tuesday · ΣΟΥΓΙΑ** | **Sunday · ΤΣΟΥΤΣΟΥΡΑΣ** |
|---|---|---|
| **Length** | **8 hours** | **3 hours** |
| **Water** | pale limestone, turquoise | **dark coves, black pebble** |
| **The point** | **ΔΩΜΑΤΑ 28** under a 600–700 m cliff | **CAVES** |

**The caves are the whole distinction, and I had been underselling them:** the
**Δ-shaped underwater cave** at Βάλα · the **πολύχρωμα sea caves at
ΤΡΑΓΟΠΙΑΣΤΗ** · caves both sides of **ΠΙΚΡΟΒΛΥΧΑΔΑ** with snorkel gear
specifically advised · **swim-through caves between ΤΡΕΙΣ ΕΚΚΛΗΣΙΕΣ and ΜΟΝΗ
ΚΟΥΔΟΥΜΑ**. **Σούγια has none of this.**

**And they are five days apart** — Tue 27 and Sun 1. Repetition bites on adjacent
days; these are not adjacent.

> **CONDITION: Sunday stays at THREE HOURS and is the cave-and-snorkel day. If
> the only offer is longer, DROP IT** — four dark-water 27s are not worth an
> eight-hour repeat.

| cretanbeaches | gives Άσπες as **4 km east of Τρεις Εκκλησιές** — **the same figure** |

## 🔴 THE REAL FINDING — THE ACCESS GRADES WERE WRONG

| Beach | I claimed | Its own page says |
|---|---|---|
| **ΑΓΙΟΣ ΝΙΚΗΤΑΣ** | **4-hour easy footpath** | **«Accessibility: Dirt track, Walking»** — a **rough dirt road ~15 km from ΑΧΕΝΤΡΙΑΣ**, then **230 STEPS** down from the monastery. **The 4-hour footpath appears NOWHERE on its own page** |
| **ΜΑΡΤΣΑΛΟ** | a walk | **drive the «poor dirt track» from ΟΔΗΓΗΤΡΙΑ**, park at the gorge mouth, **then 2 km on foot** + 15–20 min through palm groves |
| **ΣΚΙΑΔΑΚΙ** | ~1 hour from Καλάμι | **«Access is by boat»** |

**Standing rule: «Where the road turns to dirt, that is the car park»** — Cretan
rental insurance excludes unpaved roads outright, **and a 4×4 does not restore
cover.**

> **«SIX AT 26 ON FOOT» WAS WRONG.**
>
> **The Asterousia is a dirt-road coast, and our own insurance rule removes most
> of the walk-ins. THE BOAT IS NOT A LUXURY THERE — IT IS THE WAY IN.**
>
> **This reverses what I wrote two sections ago and makes the Salty Trips message
> MORE important, not less.**

**Note on Άγιος Νικήτας:** the 4-hour **ΤΣΟΥΤΣΟΥΡΑΣ → ΜΑΡΙΔΑΚΙ → ΑΓΙΟΣ ΝΙΚΗΤΑΣ**
footpath came from a different source and may well be real — **but it is not on
the beach's own page, and after tonight that is not good enough to plan a day
on.** Must be confirmed independently.

## 🆕 A SECOND OPERATOR — the eastern boat is no longer a single point of failure

> **`exclusiveadventures.gr` — «Crete boat trip to ΑΣΠΕΣ and lunch in
> ΚΕΡΑΤΟΚΑΜΠΟΣ»**

**A published Άσπες route from a different company.** Κερατόκαμπος is east of
Τσούτσουρας, so this is the same coast from the other end. **Ask both.**

# 🔵 THE TURQUOISE SWEEP — EAST CONFIRMED, WEST PARTLY DONE

## ✅ EAST: THERE IS NO TURQUOISE. CONFIRMED.

**All nine structured fields read Deep blue or Blue. Not one reads Turquoise.**

**Two apparent counter-claims, both resolved against turquoise:**

| Claim | Resolution |
|---|---|
| A listicle: **ΚΑΜΙΝΑΚΙ has «shallow turquoise water»** | **The same source says «steep CLAY cliffs».** Clay suspends in the water column and **kills** turquoise — our own substrate rule. **And its own field says Blue.** |
| **ΤΡΥΠΗΤΗ (Αστερούσια): «dark pebbles and crystalline blue-green water»** | **γαλαζοπράσινο over DARK pebble** — the rule requires a **pale** substrate. **Stays 7.** |

> **VERDICT: the Asterousia is a deep-blue coast. Dramatic, clear, cave-riddled —
> and NOT turquoise. Zac's rule applies: if the competition is immense, the east
> does not justify itself on colour.**

## 🔄 WEST: ONE UP, TWO OUT

### ⬆️ ΚΕΔΡΟΔΑΣΟΣ 26 → **27** — a genuine Colour 9

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **Sea water color** | **TURQUOISE** ✅ |
| **Depth** | **SHALLOW** ✅ |
| **Sand type** | **WHITE SAND**, rocks in places ✅ |
| Accessibility | Dirt track, **Walking** — **the E4 crosses the beach** |

> **The complete mechanism: turquoise field + shallow + white sand.**
> **ΚΕΔΡΟΔΑΣΟΣ `8 · 9 · 10` = 27.**
>
> **It joins ΜΠΑΛΟΣ and ΕΛΑΦΟΝΗΣΙ as a Cretan Colour 9 — and unlike them it has
> no coach park, only the E4 footpath.**

### ⬇️ ΓΛΥΚΑ ΝΕΡΑ 26 → **25** — out of the 26s

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **Sea water color** | **DEEP BLUE** |
| **Depth** | **Deep** |
| **Sand type** | Pebbles |

Prose: **«deep blue water color and nice pebbles»**. **ΓΛΥΚΑ ΝΕΡΑ `8·7·10` = 25.**

> ⚠️ **AND A WATER-GATE RISK:** *«Sea water is generally **cold because of the
> springs, even in midsummer**»* — fresh water gushes **up through the pebbles**.
> **In late October this may fall below the 21.5 °C gate, which would cut it
> outright.** To be checked.

### ⬇️ ΣΕΪΤΑΝ ΛΙΜΑΝΙΑ (ΣΤΕΦΑΝΟΥ) 26 → **25** — out of the 26s

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **Sea water color** | **DEEP BLUE** |
| **Depth** | **Deep** |
| **Sand type** | Fine Pebbles |

Other sources split — one says **Turquoise**, another **Dark blue**, and
crete.direct says *«pale aquamarine to deep cobalt within metres»* over **coarse
gravel and flat rock, no sand.** **The field decides: Colour 7.**

> **ΣΕΪΤΑΝ ΛΙΜΑΝΙΑ `8·7·10` = 25 — and Group 6 therefore disappears**, since it
> was that group's only entry.

ΠΡΕΒΕΛΗ · ΤΡΥΠΗΤΗ (west) · ΣΕΝΤΟΝΙ · ΛΙΣΣΟΣ**

# 🔄 THE METHOD CORRECTED, AND THE EAST RE-RUN

## THE ERROR IN THE METHOD

**I was treating cretanbeaches' structured «Χρώμα νερού» as a verdict. It is ONE
WORD FOR A WHOLE BAY.** For a beach with a deep channel and a shallow pale edge,
that word picks the body of water and **throws the shore away.**

> **THE SHORE IS WHERE WE SWIM.** This is exactly what Zac said about ΔΩΜΑΤΑ —
> *«turquoise by the sand»* — and I resisted it twice.

## THE REPLACEMENT: PREPONDERANCE + MECHANISM

**A colour grade needs all three:**
1. **Several INDEPENDENT sources** naming the colour
2. **on pages about THAT beach** *(not a neighbour's page — the Αλυκαπούντα failure)*
3. **a physical mechanism that permits it** *(a substrate that is not stated to be dark)*

**The field becomes one vote, not the verdict.**
**The discriminator is whether the substrate colour is STATED.**

## ⬅️ WEST, CORRECTED

| Beach | Evidence | New |
|---|---|---|
| **ΣΕΪΤΑΝ ΛΙΜΑΝΙΑ** | several guides **turquoise** · one **«milky-turquoise»** · *«a narrow fjord-like cove cut into **WHITE LIMESTONE** cliffs»* · field dissents · pebble colour disputed | **`8·9·10` = 27** ✅ |
| **ΓΛΥΚΑ ΝΕΡΑ** | **«γαλαζοπράσινα δροσερά νερά»** · **«κρυστάλινο-πράσινο χρώμα»** · field Βαθύ μπλε | **`8·9·10` = 27** ✅ ⚠️ **water-gate risk — cold from the springs** |

## ➡️ EAST, RE-RUN — THREE OF FOUR RESTORED

### ✅ ΑΓΙΟΦΑΡΑΓΓΟ `9 · 9 · 10` = 28

| Source | Says |
|---|---|
| exploringgreece **headline** | **«1,5 χλμ. ψιλό βοτσαλάκι και ΤΙΡΚΟΥΑΖ νερά»** |
| second | *turquoise waters that look completely fake* |
| third | *turquoise waters of the Libyan* |
| cretanbeaches field | Deep blue — **the lone outlier** |

**Fine pebble, in a limestone gorge. Substrate colour NOT stated as dark.**
**AND ITS WALK IS THE ONE THAT WAS CONFIRMED — 40 min, easy, flat gorge floor.**

> **The single best outcome of the re-run: a 28 on an easy 40-minute walk.**

### ✅ ΜΑΡΤΣΑΛΟ `9 · 9 · 10` = 28

> **patris.gr: «Κρυστάλλινα, ΓΑΛΑΖΟΠΡΑΣΙΝΑ νερά και φοίνικες!»**

Field Βαθύ μπλε; another καταγάλανα. **Blue-green attested on a dedicated
article.** **Access better than logged: 20–40 min on foot through the gorge from
ΜΟΝΗ ΟΔΗΓΗΤΡΙΑΣ**, several sources agreeing · caïque from Καλοί Λιμένες or Μάταλα.

### ✅ ΑΓΙΟΣ ΝΙΚΗΤΑΣ `9 · 9 · 10` = 28 — colour only

> cretazine: **«το βοτσαλάκι και τα βαθιά ΓΑΛΑΖΟΠΡΑΣΙΝΑ νερά»**
> second: **«πεντακάθαρα ΠΡΑΣΙΝΑ νερά»**
> its own prose: *«crystal clear and **deep green** waters»*

**Three say green or blue-green; only the field says deep blue.**

> ⚠️ **ACCESS UNCHANGED AND STILL BAD: 15 km rough χωματόδρομος from ΑΧΕΝΤΡΙΑΣ,
> then 230 STEPS through the monastery.** The insurance rule makes the dirt road a
> no. **Restored on colour, blocked on access** unless the Τσούτσουρας footpath
> checks out. *(Nudism forbidden — it is a working monastery.)*

### ❌ ΣΚΙΑΔΑΚΙ — HELD AT COLOUR 5. NEEDS ZAC'S SATELLITE READ

| Source | Says |
|---|---|
| **neakriti** | the pebble is **ΛΕΥΚΟ — WHITE** ✅ a pale substrate |
| **ruralgreece** | the water is **«βαθιά καταγάλανα»** — deep, deep blue |
| **anywhere** | **NO source names τιρκουάζ or γαλαζοπράσινο** |

> **White floor + deep-blue water is the one combination the method cannot
> resolve.** The substrate says the shallow edge should go turquoise; every actual
> colour word says it does not.
>
> 🔭 **ZAC: this is the one to read off the satellite tile. If the shore strip is
> pale, ΣΚΙΑΔΑΚΙ is a 9 and a 28.**

### ❌ ΑΣΠΕΣ — STAYS CUT, AND IT IS CONSISTENT

**Άσπες' own page DOES say «vivid turquoise».** Overridden on one ground: the
substrate is **«μαύρο χαλικάκι»** — **explicitly black. Turquoise over black is
physically impossible.**

> **The test is whether the substrate colour is STATED.**
> **ΑΓΙΟΦΑΡΑΓΓΟ: not stated, limestone gorge → attested turquoise STANDS.**
> **ΑΣΠΕΣ: stated, and black → it FALLS.**
> **Same test, opposite results, no thumb on the scale.**
